[viewers/smarty/showReps.php](viewers/smarty/showReps.php)\n- [viewers/smarty/updateReportOutput.php](viewers/smarty/updateReportOutput.php)\n\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n\n\n## Purpose and Scope\n\nThis document provides a comprehensive overview of the Heurist academic knowledge management system, a web-based platform for managing scholarly databases and digital humanities projects. This page covers the system's core architecture, primary components, and fundamental design patterns that enable researchers to create, manage, and publish structured data collections.\n\nFor specific information about individual subsystems, see [System Architecture](#2) for detailed component documentation, [User Interface](#3) for client-side interface details, [Data Management](#4) for import/export capabilities, [Advanced Features](#5) for specialized tools like mapping and CMS, and [Administration](#6) for system management functions.\n\n## System Architecture Overview\n\nHeurist is a full-stack web application built on PHP/MySQL backend with a jQuery-based frontend. The system follows a modular architecture with clear separation between data management, user interface, and specialized features like mapping and content management.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n standardized server communication and event management.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph LR\n    subgraph \"Client-Server Communication\"\n        HAPI_CALLSERVER[\"_callserver()\u003cbr/\u003eAJAX wrapper\"]\n        CONTROLLERS[\"PHP Controllers\u003cbr/\u003erecord_edit.php\u003cbr/\u003eentityScrud.php\"]\n        RESPONSE[\"JSON responses\u003cbr/\u003eStatus \u0026 data\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph Heurist to function as both a database management system for researchers and a platform for publishing scholarly content to the web, with extensive customization capabilities for domain-specific workflows in digital humanities and academic research."])self.__next_f.push([1,"15:T3c74,"])self.__next_f.push([1,"# System Architecture\n\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003eRelevant source [movetoparent/heuristConfigIni.php](movetoparent/heuristConfigIni.php)\n\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n\n\nThis document provides an overview of the core architecture of the Heurist academic knowledge management system. It covers the fundamental system components, initialization processes, configuration management, and data flow patterns that form the foundation of the application.\n\nFor information about user interface components and widgets, see [User Interface](#3). For database schema and structure details, see [Database Structure](#2.3). For specific functional modules like import/export, see [Data Management](#4).\n\n## Overview\n\nHeurist follows a layered architecture with a clear separation between server-side PHP components and client-side JavaScript. The system is built around a central `System` class that manages database connections, user sessions, configuration, and core services. The architecture emphasizes modularity, with distinct layers for configuration management, database access, user interface, and business logic.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"Client Layer\"\n        Browser[\"Browser\"]\n        HAPI[\"HAPI (hapi.js)\"]\n        Widgets[\"jQuery Widgets\"]\n        UI[\"User Interface\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Server Layer\"\n        SystemClass[\"System Class\u003cbr/\u003e(hserv/System.php)\"]\n        Config[\"Configuration\u003cbr/\u003e(configIni.php)\"]\n        Database[\"Database Layer\u003cbr/\u003e(utils_db.php)\"]\n        Controllers[\"Controllers\u003cbr/\u003e(hserv/controller/)\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Data Layer\"\n        MySQL[\"MySQL Database\"]\n        Files[\"File Storage\"]\n    end\n    \n    Browser --\u003e HAPI\n    HAPI --\u003e Widgets\n    Widgets --\u003e UI\n    HAPI --\u003e Controllers\n    Controllers --\u003e SystemClass\n    SystemClass --\u003e Config\n    SystemClass --\u003e Database\n    Database --\u003e MySQL\n    SystemClass --\u003e Files\n```\n\n**System Architecture Overview**\n\nSources: [hserv/System.php:1-600](), [hclient/framecontent/initPage.php:1-100](), [configIni.php:1-200]()\n\n## Core System Initialization\n\nThe system initialization follows a well-defined sequence that establishes the runtime environment, database connections, and user sessions. This process is coordinated by the `System` class and page initialization scripts.\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant Client as \"Client Browser\"\n    participant initPage as \"initPage.php\"\n    participant SystemClass as \"System Class\"\n    participant Config as \"configIni.php\"\n    participant Database as \"MySQL\"\n    participant HAPI as \"HAPI (Client)\"\n    \n    Client-\u003e\u003einitPage: \"Request Page\"\n    initPage-\u003e\u003eSystemClass: \"new System()\"\n    SystemClass-\u003e\u003eConfig: \"Load Configuration\"\n    Config--\u003e\u003eSystemClass: \"Config Values\"\n    SystemClass-\u003e\u003eDatabase: \"mysql__init()\"\n    Database--\u003e\u003eSystemClass: \"Connection\"\n    SystemClass-\u003e\u003eSystemClass: \"initPathConstants()\"\n    SystemClass-\u003e\u003eSystemClass: \"loginVerify()\"\n    initPage-\u003e\u003eClient: \"HTML + JavaScript\"\n    Client-\u003e\u003eHAPI: \"Initialize HAPI\"\n    HAPI-\u003e\u003eSystemClass: \"API Requests\"\n```\n\n**System Initialization Sequence**\n\nThe initialization process involves several key phases:\n\n1. **Configuration Loading**: The system reads configuration from `configIni.php` and optionally from a parent directory override file\n2. **Database Connection**: Establishes MySQL connection using parameters from configuration\n3. **Path Constants**: Defines file system paths for data storage and temporary files\n4. **Session Management**: Verifies or establishes user sessions\n5. **Client Initialization**: Loads JavaScript API and initializes client-side components\n\nSources: [hclient/framecontent/initPage.php:39-50](), [hserv/System.php:144-192](), [configIni.php:200-207]()\n\n## Configuration Architecture\n\nHeurist implements a hierarchical configuration system that allows for instance-specific overrides while maintaining system-wide defaults. This design supports multiple Heurist installations on a single server with shared configuration.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TD\n    ParentConfig[\"../heuristConfigIni.php\u003cbr/\u003e(Server-wide)\"]\n    InstanceConfig[\"configIni.php\u003cbr/\u003e(Instance-specific)\"]\n    SystemClass[\"System Class\"]\n    Constants[\"Runtime Constants\"]\n    \n    ParentConfig --\u003e InstanceConfig\n    InstanceConfig --\u003e SystemClass\n    SystemClass --\u003e Constants\n    \n    ParentConfig -.-\u003e |\"Provides defaults\"| SystemClass\n    InstanceConfig -.-\u003e |\"Overrides if set\"| SystemClass\n```\n\n**Configuration Hierarchy**\n\nThe configuration system handles essential parameters including:\n\n- **Database Connection**: Host, credentials, port, and prefix settings\n- **File Storage**: Paths for uploaded files, thumbnails, and system directories  \n- **Security**: Passwords for administrative functions and access control\n- **External Services**: HTTP proxy settings and third-party service configurations\n- **Server Settings**: Base URLs, email domains, and instance identification\n\nKey configuration variables include `$dbHost`, `$dbAdminUsername`, `$defaultRootFileUploadPath`, and `$serverName`, which are processed during system initialization.\n\nSources: [configIni.php:99-207](), [hserv/System.php:753-800](), [movetoparent/heuristConfigIni.php:1-50]()\n\n## Database Access Layer\n\nThe database access layer provides a consistent interface for MySQL operations through a set of utility functions prefixed with `mysql__`. This abstraction handles connection management, query execution, and transaction control.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"Application Layer\"\n        SystemClass[\"System Class\"]\n        RecordMgr[\"Record Management\"]\n        EntityMgr[\"Entity Management\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Database Access Layer\"\n        mysql__init[\"mysql__init()\"]\n        mysql__select[\"mysql__select()\"]\n        mysql__insertupdate[\"mysql__insertupdate()\"]\n        mysql__transaction[\"mysql__begin_transaction()\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Database Layer\"\n        MySQLServer[\"MySQL Server\"]\n        HeuristDB[\"Heurist Database\"]\n    end\n    \n    SystemClass --\u003e mysql__init\n    RecordMgr --\u003e mysql__select\n    RecordMgr --\u003e mysql__insertupdate\n    EntityMgr --\u003e mysql__transaction\n    \n    mysql__init --\u003e MySQLServer\n    mysql__select --\u003e HeuristDB\n    mysql__insertupdate --\u003e HeuristDB\n    mysql__transaction --\u003e HeuristDB\n```\n\n**Database Access Architecture**\n\nThe database layer provides several categories of functions:\n\n- **Connection Management**: `mysql__init()`, `mysql__connection()`, `mysql__usedatabase()`\n- **Query Operations**: `mysql__select()`, `mysql__select_assoc()`, `mysql__select_value()`\n- **Data Modification**: `mysql__insertupdate()`, `mysql__delete()`, `mysql__exec_param_query()`\n- **Transaction Control**: `mysql__begin_transaction()`, transaction rollback and commit\n- **Database Administration**: `mysql__create_database()`, `mysql__drop_database()`\n\nThis layer ensures consistent error handling, parameter sanitization, and connection pooling across the application.\n\nSources: [hserv/dbaccess/utils_db.php:77-450](), [hserv/System.php:152-161](), [hserv/dbaccess/utils_db.php:87-102]()\n\n## Constants and Magic Numbers\n\nHeurist uses a sophisticated system of constants to map human-readable identifiers to database-specific IDs. This allows the system to work across different database instances while maintaining semantic consistency.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph LR\n    subgraph \"Magic Constants\"\n        RT_PERSON[\"RT_PERSON\"]\n        DT_NAME[\"DT_NAME\"] \n        TRM_ACTIVE[\"TRM_ACTIVE\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Lookup Tables\"\n        rtDefines[\"$rtDefines\u003cbr/\u003e(Record Types)\"]\n        dtDefines[\"$dtDefines\u003cbr/\u003e(Detail Types)\"]\n        trmDefines[\"$trmDefines\u003cbr/\u003e(Terms)\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Local Database\"\n        LocalIDs[\"Local IDs\u003cbr/\u003e(defRecTypes, defDetailTypes, defTerms)\"]\n    end\n    \n    RT_PERSON --\u003e rtDefines\n    DT_NAME --\u003e dtDefines\n    TRM_ACTIVE --\u003e trmDefines\n    \n    rtDefines --\u003e LocalIDs\n    dtDefines --\u003e LocalIDs\n    trmDefines --\u003e LocalIDs\n```\n\n**Constants Resolution System**\n\nThe system maintains three types of magic constants:\n\n- **Record Type Constants**: `RT_PERSON`, `RT_ORGANISATION`, etc. mapped via `defineRTLocalMagic()`\n- **Detail Type Constants**: `DT_NAME`, `DT_START_DATE`, etc. mapped via `defineDTLocalMagic()`  \n- **Term Constants**: `TRM_ACTIVE`, `TRM_PRIVATE`, etc. mapped via `defineTermLocalMagic()`\n\nEach constant is resolved to its local database ID through lookup functions that query the `defRecTypes`, `defDetailTypes`, and `defTerms` tables respectively.\n\nSources: [hserv/System.php:218-244](), [hserv/System.php:409-461](), [hserv/consts.php:485-650]()\n\n## File System Organization\n\nThe system implements a structured file storage architecture that organizes uploaded files, system resources, and temporary data across multiple directories with specific purposes.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TD\n    Root[\"HEURIST_FILESTORE_ROOT\"]\n    DbFolder[\"Database Folder\u003cbr/\u003e(dbname/)\"]\n    \n    Root --\u003e DbFolder\n    \n    DbFolder --\u003e Uploads[\"file_uploads/\u003cbr/\u003e(User uploaded files)\"]\n    DbFolder --\u003e Thumbs[\"filethumbs/\u003cbr/\u003e(Generated thumbnails)\"]\n    DbFolder --\u003e Settings[\"settings/\u003cbr/\u003e(Configuration files)\"]\n    DbFolder --\u003e Reports[\"generated-reports/\u003cbr/\u003e(Export outputs)\"]\n    DbFolder --\u003e Scratch[\"scratch/\u003cbr/\u003e(Temporary files)\"]\n    DbFolder --\u003e Entity[\"entity/\u003cbr/\u003e(Icons, type images)\"]\n    DbFolder --\u003e Backup[\"backup/\u003cbr/\u003e(Database backups)\"]\n    DbFolder --\u003e Templates[\"smarty-templates/\u003cbr/\u003e(Report templates)\"]\n```\n\n**File System Structure**\n\nThe file organization follows these principles:\n\n- **Database Isolation**: Each database has its own directory under the filestore root\n- **Functional Separation**: Different types of files are segregated into purpose-specific folders\n- **Security Boundaries**: Some directories allow web access while others are restricted\n- **Backup Inclusion**: Core directories are included in database backups while cache directories are excluded\n\nKey path constants include `HEURIST_FILESTORE_DIR`, `HEURIST_THUMB_DIR`, `HEURIST_FILES_DIR`, and `HEURIST_SCRATCH_DIR`.\n\nSources: [hserv/System.php:606-641](), [hserv/System.php:753-800](), [hserv/System.php:655-670]()\n\n## Session and User Management\n\nThe system implements a comprehensive session management system that handles user authentication, preferences, and security across multiple browser sessions and database instances.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"Session Layer\"\n        PHPSession[\"PHP Session\"]\n        UserPrefs[\"User Preferences\"]\n        Security[\"Security Context\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"User Management\"\n        SystemClass[\"System Class\"]\n        LoginVerify[\"loginVerify()\"]\n        UserData[\"User Data Cache\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Database\"\n        sysUGrps[\"sysUGrps Table\"]\n        sysUsrGrpLinks[\"sysUsrGrpLinks Table\"]\n    end\n    \n    PHPSession --\u003e SystemClass\n    SystemClass --\u003e LoginVerify\n    LoginVerify --\u003e UserData\n    LoginVerify --\u003e sysUGrps\n    LoginVerify --\u003e sysUsrGrpLinks\n    UserData --\u003e UserPrefs\n    UserData --\u003e Security\n```\n\n**Session Management Architecture**\n\nThe session system provides:\n\n- **Authentication State**: Maintains login status and user identity across requests\n- **Permission Caching**: Stores user roles and group memberships for efficient access control\n- **Preference Management**: Handles user-specific settings and interface customizations\n- **Multi-Database Support**: Manages sessions across different Heurist database instances\n- **Security Context**: Tracks administrative privileges and access rights\n\nThe `System` class coordinates session management through methods like `loginVerify()`, `getCurrentUser()`, and session state tracking.\n\nSources: [hserv/System.php:170-189](), [hserv/structure/dbsUsersGroups.php:78-135](), `hclient/core/hapi.js:1-1200`\n\n### Server-Side System Class\n\nThe `System` class in `hserv/System.php` serves as the core server-side component responsible for:\n\n- Database connection management This system provides a flexible approach to configuration management that allows shared settings across multiple Heurist codebases while enabling instance-specific overrides when needed.\n\nFor information about database structure configuration, see [Database Structure](#2.3). For core system initialization that uses these configurations, see [Core Components](#2.1).\n\n## Configuration Hierarchy\n\nThe Heurist configuration system employs a two-level hierarchical structure designed to support multiple instances on a single server while maintaining configuration consistency and security.\n\n### Configuration Loading Process\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart TD\n    START[[\"System Startup\"]] --\u003e LOAD_INSTANCE[\"Load configIni.php\"]\n    LOAD_INSTANCE --\u003e CHECK_PARENT{\"Parent config exists?\"}\n    CHECK_PARENT --\u003e|Yes| LOAD_PARENT[\"Load ../heuristConfigIni.php\"]\n    CHECK_PARENT --\u003e|No| USE_INSTANCE[\"Use instance settings only\"]\n    LOAD_PARENT --\u003e MERGE[\"Merge configurations\"]\n    MERGE --\u003e FINAL[\"Final configuration\"]\n    USE_INSTANCE --\u003e FINAL\n    \n    MERGE --\u003e VERSION_CHECK[\"Version always from configIni.php\"]\n    VERSION_CHECK --\u003e FINAL\n```\n\n**Configuration Resolution Process**\n\nSources: [configIni.php:200-206]()\n\n### Configuration File Structure\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"Server Root\"\n        PARENT[\"heuristConfigIni.php\u003cbr/\u003eSystem-wide defaults\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Instance 1\"\n        CONFIG1[\"configIni.php\u003cbr/\u003eInstance overrides\"]\n        SYSTEM1[\"System.php\u003cbr/\u003eUses merged config\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Instance 2\"\n        CONFIG2[\"configIni.php\u003cbr/\u003eInstance overrides\"]\n        SYSTEM2[\"System.php\u003cbr/\u003eUses merged config\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Instance N\"\n        CONFIGN[\"configIni.php\u003cbr/\u003eInstance overrides\"]\n        SYSTEMN[\"System.php\u003cbr/\u003eUses merged config\"]\n    end\n    \n    PARENT --\u003e CONFIG1\n    PARENT --\u003e CONFIG2\n    PARENT --\u003e CONFIGN\n    \n    CONFIG1 --\u003e SYSTEM1\n    CONFIG2 --\u003e SYSTEM2\n    CONFIGN --\u003e SYSTEMN\n```\n\n**Multi-Instance Configuration Architecture**\n\nSources: [configIni.php:200-206](), [movetoparent/heuristConfigIni.php:26-34]()\n\n## Configuration Categories\n\nThe configuration system manages several distinct categories of settings, each serving specific system requirements.\n\n### Core System Settings\n\n| Setting Category | Key Variables | Purpose |\n|------------------|---------------|---------|\n| Version Management | `$version` | Program version (always from instance) |\n| Reference Server | `$heuristReferenceServer` | Implementation\n\nThe configuration loading process follows a specific pattern that ensures proper inheritance and override behavior.\n\n### Loading Sequence\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant App as \"Application\"\n    participant Instance as \"configIni.php\"\n    participant Parent as \"../heuristConfigIni.php\"\n    \n    App-\u003e\u003eInstance: Include configIni.php\n    Instance-\u003e\u003eInstance: Set default values\n    Instance-\u003e\u003eInstance: Check for parent config\n    Instance-\u003e\u003eParent: Include if exists\n    Parent-\u003e\u003eParent: Override empty values\n    Parent-\u003e\u003eInstance: Return control\n    Instance-\u003e\u003eApp: Configuration ready\n```\n\n**Configuration Loading Sequence**\n\nThe actual loading implementation uses PHP's conditional include mechanism:\n\n```php\n$parentIni = dirname(__FILE__).\"/../heuristConfigIni.php\";\nif (is_file($parentIni)){\n    include_once $parentIni;\n}\n```\n\nSources: [configIni.php:200-206]()\n\n### Variable Override Pattern\n\nThe parent configuration file uses a specific pattern to override only empty values:\n\n```php\nif (!@$variableName) {$variableName = \"default_value\";}\n```\n\nThis pattern ensures that:\n- Instance-specific values take precedence\n- Parent values are used only when instance values are empty\n- No fatal errors occur if variables are undefined\n\nSources: [movetoparent/heuristConfigIni.php:42-199]()\n\n## Service Integration Configuration\n\nThe configuration system manages integration with providers |\n| Analytics | `$matomoUrl`, `$matomoSiteId` | Matomo tracking |\n\nSources: [movetoparent/heuristConfigIni.php:98-214]()\n\n### Proxy Configuration\n\n```mermaid\ngraph LR\n    subgraph system includes extensive documentation and warnings about proper usage:\n\n### Critical Warnings\n\n1. **Version Control**: The `$version` variable must not be modified by users and is always determined by the development team\n2. **File Modification**: The `configIni.php` file will be overwritten during upgrades\n3. **Parent Directory**: Settings should be placed in the parent directory configuration file\n4. **Password Security**: Administrative passwords must be over 14 characters to be effective\n\n### Recommended Practices\n\n| Practice | Rationale |\n|----------|-----------|\n| Use parent directory configuration | Avoids duplication and accidental password distribution |\n| Retain `hdb_` database prefix | Enables proper database grouping and management |\n| Set server function password | Prevents server overload from analysis functions |\n| Configure file paths correctly | Ensures proper file storage and web access |\n\nSources: [configIni.php:19-73](), enables storage of diverse record types with dynamic field structures. The schema supports a key-value data model where records can have different sets of fields based on their record type, while maintaining referential integrity and supporting complex relationships.\n\nFor information about how this database structure is used by the backend API system, see [Core Components](#2.1). For details on how records are created and modified through the user interface, see [Record Editing](#3.2).\n\n## Core Database Architecture\n\nHeurist uses a flexible Entity-Attribute-Value (EAV) model that allows different record types to have completely different field structures while sharing common storage tables.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"Core Data Storage\"\n Entity-Attribute-Value pattern where each record can have multiple details (field values) of different types.\n\n### Records Table Structure\n\n| Field | Type | Purpose |\n|-------|------|---------|\n| `rec_ID` | int unsigned | Primary key |\n| `rec_Title` | varchar(1023) | Computed display title |\n| `rec_RecTypeID` | smallint unsigned | References `defRecTypes.rty_ID` |\n| `rec_OwnerUGrpID` | smallint unsigned | Owner user/group |\n| `rec_NonOwnerVisibility` | enum | Access control |\n| `rec_Added` | datetime | Creation timestamp |\n| `rec_Modified` | timestamp | Last modified timestamp |\n\n### Details Table Structure\n\n| Field | Type | Purpose |\n|-------|------|---------|\n| `dtl_ID` | int unsigned | Primary key |\n| `dtl_RecID` | int unsigned | References `Records.rec_ID` |\n| `dtl_DetailTypeID` | smallint unsigned | References `defDetailTypes.dty_ID` |\n| `dtl_Value` | text | The actual field value |\n| `dtl_UploadedFileID` | int unsigned | References `recUploadedFiles.ulf_ID` |\n| `dtl_Geo` | geometry | Geospatial data |\n\n**Sources:** [admin/setup/dbcreate/blankDBStructure.sql:55-82](), [admin/setup/dbcreate/blankDBStructure.sql:398-425]()\n\n## Schema Definition System\n\nHeurist's flexibility comes from its comprehensive schema definition system that allows administrators to define custom record types and field types.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph LR\n    subgraph \"Record Type Definition\"\n        defRecTypes[\"defRecTypes\u003cbr/\u003eRecord Type Definitions\"]\n different kinds of records. Each record type specifies:\n\n- **Title Mask (`rty_TitleMask`)**: Template for generating display titles\n- **Field Structure**: Which fields are available via `defRecStructure`\n- **Grouping**: Organization in the UI via `rty_RecTypeGroupID`\n- **Visibility**: Access control settings\n\n### Field Type Definition\n\nField types (`defDetailTypes`) define the available field types that can be used across record types:\n\n| Field Type | Purpose |\n|------------|---------|\n| `freetext` | Single-line text input |\n| `blocktext` | Multi-line text area |\n| `integer`, `float` | Numeric values |\n| `date` | Date/time values |\n| `enum` | Selection from controlled vocabulary |\n| `resource` | Pointer to another record |\n| `file` | File attachment |\n| `geo` | Geographic coordinates |\n\n**Sources:** [admin/setup/dbcreate/blankDBStructure.sql:142]()\n\n## Relationship and Linking System\n\nHeurist supports complex relationships between records through multiple mechanisms.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"Relationship Storage\"\n        recLinks[\"recLinks\u003cbr/\u003e(rl_SourceID, rl_TargetID, rl_RelationID)\"]\n Storage Mechanisms\n\n1. **Direct Resource Pointers**: `recDetails` entries with `dty_Type='resource'` store record IDs\n2. **Relationship Records**: Special records of type 1 (relationship) that link two records\n3. **Link Cache**: `recLinks` table maintains denormalized relationship data for performance\n\n### Relationship Constraints\n\nThe `defRelationshipConstraints` table defines rules for valid relationships:\n- Source and target record type restrictions\n- Relationship type (term) constraints\n- Cardinality limits\n\n**Sources:** [admin/setup/dbcreate/blankDBStructure.sql:316-335]()\n\n## Vocabulary and Taxonomy System\n\nHeurist includes a comprehensive vocabulary management system for controlled vocabularies and hierarchical `defDetailTypes` and `defRecStructure` have visibility settings\n\n## Specialized Index and Cache Tables\n\n### Date Indexing System\n\n```mermaid\ngraph LR\n    subgraph \"Date Processing\"\n uncertainty\n- Carbon-14 dates\n- Probable date ranges\n- JSON temporal objects\n\n### Cross-Database Mapping\n\nThe `defCrosswalk` table enables mapping of definition IDs between different Heurist databases:\n\n| Field | Purpose |\n|-------|---------|\n| `crw_SourcedbID` | Source database identifier |\n| `crw_SourceCode` | ID in source database |\n| `crw_DefType` | Type of definition (rectype, data integrity through an extensive trigger system:\n\n### Core Data Triggers\n\n1. **Record Triggers**: Archive changes, manage relationship records\n2. **Detail Triggers**: Update link cache, maintain date indexes\n3. **Term Triggers**: Maintain hierarchy in `defTermsLinks`\n\n### Archive System\n\nAll changes to records and details are archived in `sysArchive`:\n- **Change Tracking**: Before-change values stored as raw data\n- **User Attribution**: Change tracking by user ID\n- **Deletion Tracking**: Special handling for deleted records\n\n**Sources:** [admin/setup/dbcreate/addProceduresTriggers.sql:326-330](), [admin/setup/dbcreate/addProceduresTriggers.sql:398-402]()\n\n## System Configuration and Metadata\n\nThe `sysIdentification` table stores database-wide configuration and metadata:\n\n| Field | Purpose |\n|-------|---------|\n| `sys_dbName` | Database display name |\n| `sys_dbDescription` | Database description |\n| `sys_dbVersion` | Schema version for upgrades |\n| `sys_UploadDirectory` | File storage configuration |\n| `sys_NewRecAccess` | Default record visibility |\n\n**Sources:** [admin/setup/dbcreate/blankDBStructure.sql:786-830]()\n\nThis comprehensive schema provides the foundation for Heurist's flexible data management capabilities while maintaining referential integrity and supporting complex scholarly data requirements."])self.__next_f.push([1,"19:T313e,"])self.__next_f.push([1,"# User Interface\n\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003eRelevant source [index.php](index.php)\n- [layout_default.js](layout_default.js)\n\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n\n\nThis document provides an overview of Heurist's client-side user interface architecture, including the widget system, jQuery UI components, and overall UI design patterns. It covers the foundational structure and relationships between UI components that enable users to interact with records, perform searches, and manage data.\n\nFor detailed information about specific UI subsystems, see: [UI Framework](#3.1), [Record Editing](#3.2), [Record Viewing](#3.3), [Search Interface](#3.4), and [Batch Operations](#3.5).\n\n## UI Architecture Overview\n\nHeurist's user interface is built on a modular widget-based architecture using jQuery UI as the foundation. The system follows a hierarchical structure where specialized widgets handle different aspects of data interaction, from individual form fields to complex search interfaces.\n\n### Core UI Architecture\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"Core Foundation\"\n        HAPI[\"hAPI (hapi.js)\"]\n        UTILS[\"UI Utilities (utils_ui.js)\"]\n        STYLES[\"Global Styles (h4styles.css)\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Widget Framework\"\n        MANAGE_ENTITY[\"manageEntity (Base Widget)\"]\n        EDITING_INPUT[\"editing_input (Form Fields)\"]\n        RESULT_LIST[\"resultList (Data Display)\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Specialized Widgets\"\n        MANAGE_RECORDS[\"manageRecords\"]\n        SEARCH_FACETED[\"search_faceted\"]\n        SEARCH[\"search\"]\n        SVS_LIST[\"svs_list\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Entity Management\"\n        MANAGE_TERMS[\"manageDefTerms\"]\n        MANAGE_RECTYPES[\"manageDefRecTypes\"]\n        MANAGE_DETAILTYPES[\"manageDefDetailTypes\"]\n        MANAGE_RECSTRUCTURE[\"manageDefRecStructure\"]\n    end\n    \n    HAPI --\u003e MANAGE_ENTITY\n    UTILS --\u003e EDITING_INPUT\n    STYLES --\u003e RESULT_LIST\n    \n    MANAGE_ENTITY --\u003e MANAGE_RECORDS\n    MANAGE_ENTITY --\u003e MANAGE_TERMS\n    MANAGE_ENTITY --\u003e MANAGE_RECTYPES\n    MANAGE_ENTITY --\u003e MANAGE_DETAILTYPES\n    MANAGE_ENTITY --\u003e MANAGE_RECSTRUCTURE\n    \n    EDITING_INPUT --\u003e MANAGE_RECORDS\n    RESULT_LIST --\u003e MANAGE_RECORDS\n    SEARCH_FACETED --\u003e RESULT_LIST\n    SEARCH --\u003e RESULT_LIST\n    SVS_LIST --\u003e SEARCH_FACETED\n```\n\nSources: [hclient/core/hapi.js:23-58](), [hclient/core/utils_ui.js:88-91](), [hclient/widgets/entity/manageEntity.js:1-40](), [hclient/widgets/editing/editing_input.js:48]()\n\n### Widget Interaction Flow\n\n```mermaid\ngraph LR\n    USER[\"User Interaction\"] --\u003e WIDGET[\"Widget Event Handler\"]\n    WIDGET --\u003e HAPI[\"hAPI Request\"]\n    HAPI --\u003e SERVER[\"Server Controller\"]\n    SERVER --\u003e RESPONSE[\"Response Data\"]\n    RESPONSE --\u003e WIDGET_UPDATE[\"Widget Update\"]\n    WIDGET_UPDATE --\u003e UI_REFRESH[\"UI Refresh\"]\n    \n    subgraph \"Widget Types\"\n        SEARCH_W[\"Search Widgets\"]\n        EDIT_W[\"Editing Widgets\"]\n        VIEW_W[\"Viewing Widgets\"]\n        MANAGE_W[\"Management Widgets\"]\n    end\n    \n    WIDGET --\u003e SEARCH_W\n    WIDGET --\u003e EDIT_W\n    WIDGET --\u003e VIEW_W\n    WIDGET --\u003e MANAGE_W\n```\n\nSources: [hclient/widgets/entity/manageEntity.js:19-40](), [hclient/core/hapi.js:245-359]()\n\n## Core UI Components\n\n### Widget System\n\nThe Heurist UI is built around jQuery UI widgets that provide consistent patterns for initialization, event handling, and data management. The `manageEntity` widget serves as the base class for most entity management interfaces.\n\nKey widget characteristics:\n- **Consistent Lifecycle**: All widgets follow jQuery UI patterns for `_create`, `_init`, and `_destroy`\n- **Event-Driven**: Widgets communicate through standardized events like `ON_REC_SELECT` and `ON_REC_SEARCHSTART`\n- **Configurable Options**: Extensive option systems for customizing widget behavior\n- **Data Integration**: Direct integration with Heurist's server API through `hAPI`\n\nSources: [hclient/widgets/entity/manageEntity.js:1-200](), [hclient/core/hapi.js:1-100]()\n\n### Input Field System\n\nThe `editing_input` widget provides a unified interface for all form field types in Heurist. It handles various data types including text, numbers, dates, terms, file uploads, and record pointers.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    INPUT_WIDGET[\"editing_input Widget\"]\n    \n    subgraph \"Field Types\"\n        FREETEXT[\"freetext\"]\n        BLOCKTEXT[\"blocktext\"] \n        ENUM[\"enum (terms)\"]\n        RESOURCE[\"resource (pointers)\"]\n        FILE[\"file\"]\n        DATE[\"date\"]\n        GEO[\"geo\"]\n        RELMARKER[\"relmarker\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Features\"\n        VALIDATION[\"Validation\"]\n        REPEAT[\"Repeatability\"]\n        HELP[\"Help Text\"]\n        READONLY[\"Read-only Mode\"]\n    end\n    \n    INPUT_WIDGET --\u003e FREETEXT\n    INPUT_WIDGET --\u003e BLOCKTEXT\n    INPUT_WIDGET --\u003e ENUM\n    INPUT_WIDGET --\u003e RESOURCE\n    INPUT_WIDGET --\u003e FILE\n    INPUT_WIDGET --\u003e DATE\n    INPUT_WIDGET --\u003e GEO\n    INPUT_WIDGET --\u003e RELMARKER\n    \n    INPUT_WIDGET --\u003e VALIDATION\n    INPUT_WIDGET --\u003e REPEAT\n    INPUT_WIDGET --\u003e HELP\n    INPUT_WIDGET --\u003e READONLY\n```\n\nSources: [hclient/widgets/editing/editing_input.js:48-228](), [hclient/widgets/editing/editing_input.js:445-475]()\n\n### Data Display System\n\nThe `resultList` widget manages the display of record collections in multiple view modes:\n\n- **List View**: Tabular display with sortable columns\n- **Grid View**: Card-based layout for thumbnails\n- **Table View**: Detailed tabular format\n- **Icons View**: Icon-based display\n\nThe widget handles incremental rendering, selection management, and integration with search systems.\n\nSources: [hclient/widgets/viewers/resultList.js:39-163](), [hclient/widgets/viewers/resultList.js:590-800]()\n\n## Page Initialization and Theming\n\n### Initialization Process\n\nHeurist pages follow a standardized initialization pattern through `initPage.php`:\n\n1. **System Initialization**: Sets up server-side system context\n2. **HTML Header Generation**: Includes core scripts and stylesheets  \n3. **Client-side hAPI Setup**: Initializes the JavaScript API layer\n4. **Theme Application**: Applies user-selected themes and styling\n5. **Localization Loading**: Sets up language resources\n6. **Page-specific Initialization**: Calls custom `onPageInit` functions\n\nSources: [hclient/framecontent/initPage.php:1-50](), [index.php:213-250]()\n\n### Styling System\n\nThe styling system consists of:\n\n- **Base Styles**: Core layout and component styles in `h4styles.css`\n- **Theme Variations**: User-customizable themes through `initPageTheme.php`\n- **Widget-specific Styles**: Component-level styling integrated with widgets\n- **Responsive Design**: Adaptive layouts for different screen sizes\n\nSources: [h4styles.css:1-50](), [hclient/framecontent/initPageTheme.php:1-30]()\n\n## Widget Categories\n\n### Search and Navigation Widgets\n\nSearch functionality is distributed across several specialized widgets:\n\n- **`search`**: Main search interface with domain selection and query input\n- **`search_faceted`**: Advanced faceted search with drill-down capabilities  \n- **`search_faceted_wiz`**: Wizard for configuring faceted searches\n- **`svs_list`**: Management of saved searches and filters\n\nSources: [hclient/widgets/search/search.js:32-76](), [hclient/widgets/search/search_faceted.js:142-185](), [hclient/widgets/search/svs_list.js:29-103]()\n\n### Record Management Widgets\n\nRecord interaction is handled by:\n\n- **`manageRecords`**: Primary record editing and management interface\n- **`resultList`**: Display and selection of record collections\n- **`resultListMenu`**: Context menus for record actions\n\nSources: [hclient/widgets/entity/manageRecords.js:41-72](), [hclient/widgets/viewers/resultList.js:39-100]()\n\n### Definition Management Widgets\n\nDatabase structure management through:\n\n- **`manageDefRecTypes`**: Record type definitions\n- **`manageDefDetailTypes`**: Field type definitions  \n- **`manageDefTerms`**: Vocabulary and term management\n- **`manageDefRecStructure`**: Record type structure configuration\n\nSources: [hclient/widgets/entity/manageDefRecTypes.js:1-30](), [hclient/widgets/entity/manageDefDetailTypes.js:1-30](), [hclient/widgets/entity/manageDefTerms.js:1-50]()\n\n## Event System and Communication\n\nWidgets communicate through a centralized event system managed by `hAPI`. Key events include:\n\n- **`ON_REC_SEARCHSTART`**: Initiated when a search begins\n- **`ON_REC_SEARCH_FINISH`**: Fired when search results are available\n- **`ON_REC_SELECT`**: Triggered when records are selected\n- **`ON_STRUCTURE_CHANGE`**: Fired when database structure is modified\n\nThis event-driven architecture allows widgets to respond to changes without tight coupling.\n\nSources: [hclient/core/hapi.js:270-548](), [hclient/widgets/viewers/resultList.js:270-350]()\n\n## Future Considerations\n\nThe UI system is designed for extensibility and maintainability:\n\n- **Widget Modularity**: New widgets can be added following established patterns\n- **Theme Customization**: Styling can be modified without affecting functionality\n- **Event Integration**: New components can integrate with existing event flows\n- **Responsive Design**: Layout adapts to different screen sizes and devices\n\nSources: [layout_default.js:1-50](), [hclient/widgets/entity/manageEntity.js:1-100]()"])self.__next_f.push([1,"1a:T37d5,"])self.__next_f.push([1,"# UI Framework\n\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003eRelevant source initialization, menu systems, theme management, and dialog components for the Heurist interface. For information about specific record editing widgets, see [Record Editing](#3.2). For search interface components, see [Search Interface](#3.4).\n\n## Architecture Overview\n\nThe Heurist UI framework is built on jQuery UI with custom styling and a modular widget architecture. It provides standardized initialization, theming, menu systems, and dialog components used throughout the application.\n\n### Core Framework Components\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    initPage[\"initPage.php\"] --\u003e coreComponents[\"Core UI Components\"]\n    \n    coreComponents --\u003e menuSystem[\"Menu System\"]\n    coreComponents --\u003e themeSystem[\"Theme System\"] \n    coreComponents --\u003e dialogSystem[\"Dialog Framework\"]\n    coreComponents --\u003e layoutSystem[\"Layout Management\"]\n    \n    menuSystem --\u003e resultListMenu[\"resultListMenu.js\"]\n    menuSystem --\u003e exportMenu[\"exportMenu.js\"]\n    \n    themeSystem --\u003e initPageTheme[\"initPageTheme.php\"]\n    themeSystem --\u003e h6styles[\"h6styles.css\"]\n    \n    dialogSystem --\u003e recordAction[\"recordAction.js\"]\n    dialogSystem --\u003e publishDialog[\"publishDialog.js\"]\n    \n    layoutSystem --\u003e jqueryLayout[\"jQuery Layout\"]\n    layoutSystem --\u003e customWidgets[\"Custom Widget System\"]\n```\n\n**Sources:** [hclient/framecontent/initPage.php:1-511](), [h6styles.css:1-298](), [hclient/framecontent/initPageTheme.php:1-644]()\n\n## Page Initialization System\n\nThe `initPage.php` script provides standardized initialization for all Heurist pages, handling system setup, access control, and client-side framework loading.\n\n### Initialization Flow\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TD\n    initPageStart[\"initPage.php Entry\"] --\u003e systemInit[\"new hserv\\System()\"]\n    systemInit --\u003e dbInit[\"system.init(@$_REQUEST['db'])\"]\n    dbInit --\u003e accessCheck[\"Access Control Validation\"]\n    accessCheck --\u003e htmlOutput[\"HTML Header Generation\"]\n    htmlOutput --\u003e jsIncludes[\"Core JavaScript Loading\"]\n    jsIncludes --\u003e hapiSetup[\"HAPI Client Setup\"]\n    hapiSetup --\u003e onPageInit[\"onPageInit() Callback\"]\n    \n    hapiSetup --\u003e hapiCallback[\"onHapiInit()\"]\n    hapiCallback --\u003e applyTheme[\"applyTheme()\"]\n    hapiCallback --\u003e loadDefs[\"initialLoadDatabaseDefintions()\"]\n    loadDefs --\u003e userCallback[\"User onPageInit()\"]\n```\n\nThe initialization process includes several key steps:\n\n| Step | Component | Purpose |\n|------|-----------|---------|\n| System Init | `hserv\\System` | Database connection and core setup |\n| Access Control | Various `*_REQUIRED` constants | Permission validation |\n| Core JS Loading | jQuery, HAPI, utilities | Client framework setup |\n| Theme Application | `applyTheme()` | UI styling setup |\n| Database Definitions | `initialLoadDatabaseDefintions()` | Entity metadata loading |\n\n**Sources:** [hclient/framecontent/initPage.php:24-511](), [hclient/framecontent/initPageCss.php:1-44]()\n\n## Menu System Architecture\n\nThe framework provides context-sensitive dropdown menus through the `resultListMenu` widget and related components.\n\n### Menu Component Structure\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    resultListMenu[\"resultListMenu Widget\"] --\u003e divMainMenuItems[\"divMainMenuItems (ul.horizontalmenu)\"]\n    \n    divMainMenuItems --\u003e btnSelected[\"btn_Selected\"]\n    divMainMenuItems --\u003e btnCollected[\"btn_Collected\"] \n    divMainMenuItems --\u003e btnRecode[\"btn_Recode\"]\n    divMainMenuItems --\u003e btnShared[\"btn_Shared\"]\n    divMainMenuItems --\u003e btnReorder[\"btn_Reorder\"]\n    \n    btnSelected --\u003e menuSelected[\"menu_Selected\"]\n    btnCollected --\u003e menuCollected[\"menu_Collected\"]\n    btnRecode --\u003e menuRecode[\"menu_Recode\"]\n    btnShared --\u003e menuShared[\"menu_Shared\"]\n    \n    menuSelected --\u003e selectedTemplate[\"resultListMenuSelected.html\"]\n    menuCollected --\u003e collectedTemplate[\"resultListMenuCollected.html\"]\n    menuRecode --\u003e recodeTemplate[\"resultListMenuRecode.html\"]\n    menuShared --\u003e sharedTemplate[\"resultListMenuShared.html\"]\n```\n\n### Menu Action Handling\n\nThe `menuActionHandler` function in `resultListMenu` processes all menu clicks and dispatches to appropriate actions:\n\n```mermaid\ngraph LR\n    menuClick[\"Menu Item Click\"] --\u003e menuActionHandler[\"menuActionHandler(action)\"]\n    \n    menuActionHandler --\u003e selectedActions[\"Selected Actions\"]\n    menuActionHandler --\u003e collectedActions[\"Collected Actions\"]\n    menuActionHandler --\u003e recodeActions[\"Recode Actions\"]\n    \n    selectedActions --\u003e selectAll[\"menu-selected-select-all\"]\n    selectedActions --\u003e tagRecords[\"menu-selected-tag\"]\n    selectedActions --\u003e deleteRecords[\"menu-selected-delete\"]\n    \n    collectedActions --\u003e addToCollection[\"menu-collected-add\"]\n    collectedActions --\u003e showCollection[\"menu-collected-show\"]\n    \n    recodeActions --\u003e addDetail[\"menu-selected-value-add\"]\n    recodeActions --\u003e replaceDetail[\"menu-selected-value-replace\"]\n    recodeActions --\u003e changeRectype[\"menu-selected-rectype-change\"]\n```\n\n**Sources:** [hclient/widgets/viewers/resultListMenu.js:36-1338](), [hclient/widgets/viewers/resultListMenuSelected.html:1-76](), [hclient/widgets/viewers/resultListMenuRecode.html:1-94]()\n\n## Theme and Styling System\n\nThe framework provides dynamic CSS generation with user customization through a hierarchical theme system.\n\n### Theme Generation Process\n\n```mermaid\ngraph LR\n    pageLoad[\"Page Load\"] --\u003e initPageCss[\"initPageCss.php\"]\n    \n    initPageCss --\u003e jqueryBase[\"jQuery UI Base CSS\"]\n    initPageCss --\u003e h4styles[\"h4styles.css\"]\n    initPageCss --\u003e h6check[\"Layout Check (ll parameter)\"]\n    h6check --\u003e h6styles[\"h6styles.css (if not H5Default)\"]\n    initPageCss --\u003e themeGeneration[\"initPageTheme.php\"]\n    \n    themeGeneration --\u003e userPrefs[\"User Preferences (ugr_Preferences)\"]\n    themeGeneration --\u003e siteColors[\"Site Colors (websiteRecord)\"]\n    themeGeneration --\u003e defaultColors[\"Default Color Constants\"]\n    \n    userPrefs --\u003e dynamicCSS[\"Dynamic CSS Output\"]\n    siteColors --\u003e dynamicCSS\n    defaultColors --\u003e dynamicCSS\n```\n\n### Color Scheme Architecture\n\nThe theme system supports multiple color schemes defined by CSS classes:\n\n| Scheme | CSS Class | Purpose |\n|--------|-----------|---------|\n| Main | `ui-widget-content` | Default interface elements |\n| Header | `ui-heurist-header2` | Navigation and headers |\n| Editor | `ent_wrapper.editor` | Record editing interface |\n| Design | `ui-heurist-design` | Database design tools |\n| Explore | `ui-heurist-explore` | Search and browse interface |\n| Populate | `ui-heurist-populate` | Import and data entry |\n| Publish | `ui-heurist-publish` | Export and sharing |\n| Admin | `ui-heurist-admin` | Administrative functions |\n\n**Sources:** [hclient/framecontent/initPageTheme.php:1-644](), [h6styles.css:1-298](), [hclient/framecontent/initPageCss.php:18-44]()\n\n## Dialog Framework\n\nThe framework provides standardized modal dialogs for various operations through specialized dialog classes.\n\n### Dialog Component Types\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    dialogFramework[\"Dialog Framework\"] --\u003e recordActionDialog[\"hRecordAction\"]\n    dialogFramework --\u003e publishDialog[\"HPublishDialog\"]\n    dialogFramework --\u003e exportDialog[\"hexportMenu\"]\n    \n    recordActionDialog --\u003e batchOperations[\"Batch Operations\"]\n    batchOperations --\u003e rectypeChangeAction[\"rectype_change\"]\n    batchOperations --\u003e extractPdfAction[\"extract_pdf\"]\n    batchOperations --\u003e urlToFileAction[\"url_to_file\"]\n    \n    publishDialog --\u003e embedGeneration[\"Embed Code Generation\"]\n    publishDialog --\u003e urlSharing[\"URL Sharing\"]\n    \n    exportDialog --\u003e formatSelection[\"Export Format Selection\"]\n    formatSelection --\u003e csvFormat[\"CSV Export\"]\n    formatSelection --\u003e xmlFormat[\"XML/HML Export\"]\n    formatSelection --\u003e jsonFormat[\"JSON Export\"]\n```\n\n### Record Action Dialog Workflow\n\nThe `hRecordAction` class handles batch operations on records:\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TD\n    actionInit[\"hRecordAction Constructor\"] --\u003e the comprehensive record editing system in Heurist, including the core form widgets, field type handling, validation mechanisms, and the complete editing workflow. The record editing system enables users to create, modify, and manage structured data through dynamic forms that adapt to different record types and field configurations.\n\nFor information about record viewing and display, see [Record Viewing](#3.3). For search interface details, see [Search Interface](#3.4).\n\n## Architecture Overview\n\nThe record editing system is built around a layered architecture with three main components: the `editing_input` widget for individual fields, the `HEditing` class for form management, and the `manageRecords` widget for the overall record management interface.\n\n### Core Components Architecture\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"User Interface Layer\"\n        MR[\"manageRecords widget\u003cbr/\u003e$.heurist.manageRecords\"]\n        EF[\"Edit Form Dialog\u003cbr/\u003eeditFormDialog\"]\n        TB[\"Toolbar\u003cbr/\u003e_toolbar\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Form Management Layer\"\n        HE[\"HEditing class\u003cbr/\u003ewindow.HEditing\"]\n        FG[\"Form Generation\u003cbr/\u003e_renderFormField\"]\n        VL[\"Validation\u003cbr/\u003eisValid()\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Field Widget Layer\"\n        EI[\"editing_input widget\u003cbr/\u003e$.heurist.editing_input\"]\n        FT[\"Field Types\u003cbr/\u003efreetext,enum,resource,etc\"]\n        DV[\"Data Validation\u003cbr/\u003e_setValue,getValues\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Data Layer\"\n        RS[\"Record Structure\u003cbr/\u003e$Db.rst()\"]\n        DT[\"Detail Types\u003cbr/\u003e$Db.dty()\"]\n        RD[\"Record Data\u003cbr/\u003eHRecordSet\"]\n    end\n    \n    MR --\u003e HE\n    HE --\u003e EI\n    EI --\u003e FT\n    HE --\u003e FG\n    HE --\u003e VL\n    EI --\u003e DV\n    \n    HE --\u003e RS\n    EI --\u003e DT\n    MR --\u003e RD\n```\n\nSources: [hclient/widgets/editing/editing_input.js:48-396](), [hclient/widgets/entity/manageRecords.js:41-119](), [hclient/widgets/editing/editing2.js:1-57]()\n\n## Form Widget System\n\nThe `editing_input` widget is the fundamental building block of the record editing system. Each field in a record form is rendered using this widget, which adapts its behavior based on the field type and configuration.\n\n### editing_input Widget Configuration\n\n```mermaid\ngraph LR\n    subgraph \"Widget Options\"\n        DI[\"dtID\u003cbr/\u003efield identifier\"]\n        RTI[\"rectypeID\u003cbr/\u003erecord type\"]\n        DF[\"dtFields\u003cbr/\u003efield configuration\"]\n        VL[\"values\u003cbr/\u003ecurrent data\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Widget State\"\n        DT[\"detailType\u003cbr/\u003eeffective field type\"]\n        CM[\"configMode\u003cbr/\u003efield settings\"]\n        NV[\"newvalues\u003cbr/\u003ecomplex type values\"]\n        RO[\"readonly\u003cbr/\u003eedit state\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Rendered Components\"\n        HD[\"header\u003cbr/\u003efield label\"]\n        IC[\"input_cell\u003cbr/\u003einput container\"]\n        BA[\"btn_add\u003cbr/\u003erepeat button\"]\n        EM[\"error_message\u003cbr/\u003evalidation errors\"]\n    end\n    \n    DI --\u003e DT\n    RTI --\u003e DF\n    DF --\u003e CM\n    VL --\u003e NV\n    \n    DT --\u003e HD\n    CM --\u003e IC\n    DF --\u003e BA\n    VL --\u003e EM\n```\n\nThe widget initialization process loads field definitions from the database structure and creates appropriate input controls:\n\n```javascript\n// Key initialization from editing_input.js\n_create: function() {\n    if(this.options.dtFields==null \u0026\u0026 this.options.dtID\u003e0 \u0026\u0026 this.options.rectypeID\u003e0) {\n        this.options.dtFields = window.hWin.HEURIST4.util.cloneJSON($Db.rst(this.options.rectypeID, this.options.dtID));\n    }\n    \n    this.detailType = this.options.detailtype ? this.options.detailtype : this.f('dty_Type');\n    this.configMode = this.f('rst_FieldConfig');\n}\n```\n\nSources: [hclient/widgets/editing/editing_input.js:396-481](), [hclient/widgets/editing/editing_input.js:48-228]()\n\n## Field Types and Data Handling\n\nThe editing system supports multiple field types, each with specialized input controls and validation logic. The system maps database field type definitions to appropriate UI widgets.\n\n### Field Type Mapping\n\n| Field Type | Widget | Description |\n|------------|--------|-------------|\n| `freetext` | Text input | Single-line text with optional entry masks |\n| `blocktext` | Textarea | Multi-line text with TinyMCE integration |\n| `enum` | Select/Radio/Checkbox | Term selection from vocabularies |\n| `resource` | Record pointer | Links to other records |\n| `file` | File upload | File attachments and media |\n| `date` | Date picker | Temporal data with various formats |\n| `geo` | Geospatial input | Geographic coordinates and shapes |\n| `float` | Numeric input | Decimal numbers with validation |\n\n### Field Widget Lifecycle\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant HE as \"HEditing\"\n    participant EI as \"editing_input\"\n    participant DB as \"$Db structure\"\n    participant UI as \"DOM\"\n    \n    HE-\u003e\u003eDB: rst(rectypeID, dtID)\n    DB-\u003e\u003eHE: field definition\n    HE-\u003e\u003eEI: create widget\n    EI-\u003e\u003eDB: load field config\n    EI-\u003e\u003eUI: render input controls\n    EI-\u003e\u003eEI: bind event handlers\n    EI-\u003e\u003eHE: widget ready\n    \n    Note over EI,UI: User interaction\n    UI-\u003e\u003eEI: input change\n    EI-\u003e\u003eEI: validate input\n    EI-\u003e\u003eHE: trigger change event\n```\n\nThe field creation process involves several key steps implemented in the `_addInputElements` method:\n\nSources: [hclient/widgets/editing/editing_input.js:1275-1400](), [hclient/core/utils_dbs.js:110-130]()\n\n## Record Management Interface\n\nThe `manageRecords` widget provides the top-level interface for record editing, handling the overall editing workflow, editing workflow involves multiple stages from record selection through validation and saving.\n\n### Complete Editing Process\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart TD\n    Start([User initiates edit]) --\u003e LR[Load record data]\n    LR --\u003e CR[Create HEditing instance]\n    CR --\u003e GF[Generate form fields]\n    GF --\u003e RF[Render editing_input widgets]\n    RF --\u003e BD[Bind event --\u003e|No| SE[Show errors]\n    VR --\u003e|Yes| SM[Mark modified]\n    \n    SE --\u003e SF\n    SM --\u003e US{User saves?}\n    \n    US --\u003e|No| SF\n    US --\u003e|Yes| FV[Final SRR --\u003e|Error| SE\n    SRR --\u003e|Success| UR[Update record cache]\n    UR --\u003e TC[Trigger change events]\n    TC --\u003e End([Edit complete])\n```\n\n### Key Workflow Methods\n\nThe **Field Rendering**: `editing_input._create()`\n4. **Validation**: `HEditing.isValid()` and `editing_input._isValid()`\n5. **Data Saving**: `manageRecords._saveEditAndClose()`\n\nSources: [hclient/widgets/entity/manageRecords.js:1200-1400](), [hclient/widgets/editing/editing2.js:200-400](), [hclient/widgets/editing/editing_input.js:3000-3200]()\n\n## Integration Points\n\nThe record [viewers/record/renderRecordData.php:503-678]()\n\n## Core Architecture\n\nThe record viewing system follows a server-side rendering approach with client-side enhancement for interactive features. The main components include data retrieval, formatting, media handling, and user interaction management.\n\n### Viewing Modes and Configuration\n\nThe system determines viewing behavior through request parameters and internal configuration variables:\n\n| Parameter | Variable | Purpose |\n|-----------|----------|---------|\n| `mapPopup=1` | `$is_map_popup` | Simplified display for map overlays |\n| `noheader=1` | `$without_header` | Removes header elements |\n| `noclutter` | `$noclutter` | Minimal interface elements |\n| `ll=WebSearch` | `$is_production` | Public website integration |\n| `privateDetails` | `$show_private_details` | Privacy display control (0-2) |\n| `hideImages` | `$hide_images` | Media visibility control |\n| `fontsize` | `$usr_font_size` | Font size customization |\n\n**Configuration Flow**\n\n```mermaid\ngraph LR\n    subgraph \"Request Parameters\"\n        mapPopup[\"mapPopup\"]\n        noheader[\"noheader\"] \n The system consists of frontend widgets for query construction and a sophisticated backend query processing pipeline.\n\n**Search System Architecture**\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"Frontend [hserv/controller/record_batch.php](hserv/controller/record_batch.php)\n\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n\n\n## Purpose and Scope\n\nThe Batch Operations system enables users to perform bulk modifications on multiple records simultaneously within Heurist. This system provides a comprehensive interface for field-level operations (add, replace, delete values), record type changes, file operations, and specialized text processing across selected record sets.\n\nThis document covers the client-side user interface, server-side processing, and workflow coordination for batch operations. For general record editing operations on individual records, see [Record Editing](#3.2). For import/export operations that process external data files, see [Import System](#4.1) and [Export System](#4.2).\n\n## System Architecture\n\nThe batch operations system follows a layered architecture with clear separation between UI components, API communication, and server-side processing:\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"User Interface Layer\"\n        resultListMenu[\"resultListMenu.js\u003cbr/\u003eMenu System\"]\n        recordActionDialog[\"recordAction.js\u003cbr/\u003ehRecordAction Class\"]\n        recordActionPage[\"recordAction.php\u003cbr/\u003eDialog Container\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Core Libraries\"\n        editingInput[\"editing_input.js\u003cbr/\u003eField Widgets\"]\n        hapi[\"hapi.js\u003cbr/\u003eAPI Communication\"]\n        recordModify[\"recordModify.php\u003cbr/\u003eServer Processing\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Specialized Operations\"\n        combineDuplicates[\"combineDuplicateRecords.php\u003cbr/\u003eMerge Records\"]\n        verificationScripts[\"Admin Verification\u003cbr/\u003eBatch Utilities\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Data Storage\"\n        mysqlDb[(\"MySQL Database\u003cbr/\u003eRecords \u0026 Details\")]\n        fileStore[(\"File System\u003cbr/\u003eHEURIST_FILESTORE\")]\n    end\n    \n    resultListMenu --\u003e recordActionDialog\n    recordActionDialog --\u003e recordActionPage\n    recordActionPage --\u003e editingInput\n    recordActionDialog --\u003e hapi\n    hapi --\u003e recordModify\n    recordModify --\u003e mysqlDb\n    combineDuplicates --\u003e mysqlDb\n    verificationScripts --\u003e mysqlDb\n    recordModify --\u003e fileStore\n```\n\n**Batch Operations System Architecture**\n\nThe system implements a menu-driven interface where batch actions are initiated through `resultListMenu.js`, configured in `recordAction.js`, and processed via HAPI calls to server-side handlers.\n\nSources: [hclient/widgets/viewers/resultListMenu.js:36-456](), [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:35-1298](), [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.php:52-94](), [admin/verification/combineDuplicateRecords.php:69-86]()\n\n## User Interface Components\n\n### Result List Menu System\n\nThe batch operations interface is accessed through the `resultListMenu` widget, which provides contextual dropdown menus based on record selection:\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TD\n    subgraph \"Menu Categories\"\n        btnSelected[\"btn_Selected\u003cbr/\u003eSelection Actions\"]\n        btnCollected[\"btn_Collected\u003cbr/\u003eCollection Actions\"]\n        btnRecode[\"btn_Recode\u003cbr/\u003eData Modification\"]\n        btnShared[\"btn_Shared\u003cbr/\u003eSharing Actions\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Batch Actions\"\n        selectActions[\"menu-selected-*\u003cbr/\u003eselect-all, select-none\u003cbr/\u003etag, bookmark, delete\"]\n        fieldActions[\"menu-selected-value-*\u003cbr/\u003eadd, replace, delete\"]\n        typeActions[\"menu-selected-rectype-change\u003cbr/\u003emenu-selected-merge\"]\n        fileActions[\"menu-selected-url-to-file\u003cbr/\u003emenu-selected-local-to-repository\"]\n        textActions[\"menu-selected-case-conversion\u003cbr/\u003emenu-selected-translation\"]\n    end\n    \n    btnSelected --\u003e selectActions\n    btnRecode --\u003e fieldActions\n    btnRecode --\u003e typeActions\n    btnRecode --\u003e fileActions\n    btnRecode --\u003e textActions\n    btnCollected --\u003e selectActions\n```\n\n**Result List Menu Structure**\n\nThe menu system uses the `_initMenu()` method to dynamically load HTML templates and bind click handlers via `menuActionHandler()`. Access control is enforced through `data-user-admin-status` attributes.\n\nSources: [hclient/widgets/viewers/resultListMenu.js:317-456](), [hclient/widgets/viewers/resultListMenu.js:469-743](), [hclient/widgets/viewers/resultListMenuRecode.html:20-75]()\n\n### Record Action Dialog\n\nThe `hRecordAction` class manages the popup dialog interface for batch operations:\n\n| Component | Purpose | Implementation |\n|-----------|---------|----------------|\n| `_fillSelectRecordScope()` | Populates record selection options | All, Current, Selected, Collected, By Type |\n| `_fillSelectFieldTypes()` | Creates field type selectors | Uses `createRectypeDetailSelect()` with allowed field types |\n| `_createInputElements()` | Generates action-specific input widgets | Dynamic UI based on `action_type` parameter |\n| `_startAction()` | Initiates server-side batch processing | HAPI4 API calls with validation |\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TD\n    subgraph \"hRecordAction Constructor\"\n        initParams[\"action_type, scope_type\u003cbr/\u003efield_type, field_value\"]\n        init[\"_init()\u003cbr/\u003eSetup UI elements\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Dynamic UI Generation\"\n        fillScope[\"_fillSelectRecordScope()\u003cbr/\u003eRecord selection dropdown\"]\n        fillFields[\"_fillSelectFieldTypes()\u003cbr/\u003eField type dropdown\"]\n        createInputs[\"_createInputElements()\u003cbr/\u003eAction-specific widgets\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Action Processing\"\n        startAction[\"_startAction()\u003cbr/\u003eValidate and execute\"]\n        getFieldValue[\"getFieldValue()\u003cbr/\u003eExtract form values\"]\n        apiCall[\"HAPI4 API call\u003cbr/\u003eServer processing\"]\n    end\n    \n    initParams --\u003e init\n    init --\u003e fillScope\n    fillScope --\u003e fillFields\n    fillFields --\u003e createInputs\n    createInputs --\u003e startAction\n    startAction --\u003e getFieldValue\n    getFieldValue --\u003e apiCall\n```\n\n**Record Action Dialog Flow**\n\nThe dialog dynamically adjusts its interface based on the selected `action_type` and provides real-time validation through the `_onRecordScopeChange()` method.\n\nSources: [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:35-94](), [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:133-319](), [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:724-1298](), [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.php:76-94]()\n\n## Batch Operation Types\n\n### Field Value Operations\n\nField-level batch operations allow modification of record details across multiple records:\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TD\n    subgraph \"Field Operations\"\n        addDetail[\"add_detail\u003cbr/\u003eInsert new field values\"]\n        replaceDetail[\"replace_detail\u003cbr/\u003eFind `_createInputElements()` with radio button controls for operation modes. The `_startAction()` method processes the selected options and constructs API requests.\n\nSources: [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:330-401](), [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:755-777](), [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:394-400]()\n\n### Record Type Changes\n\nThe `rectype_change` operation supports bulk conversion of records from one type to another:\n\n```mermaid\ngraph LR\n    subgraph \"UI Components\"\n        divSelRectype[\"div_sel_rectype\u003cbr/\u003eTarget type selector\"]\n        selRecordtype[\"sel_recordtype\u003cbr/\u003eRecord type dropdown\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Processing\"\n        fillSelectRecordTypes[\"_fillSelectRecordTypes()\u003cbr/\u003ePopulate dropdown\"]\n        createRectypeSelect[\"createRectypeSelect()\u003cbr/\u003eUI helper\"]\n        startAction[\"_startAction()\u003cbr/\u003eExecute conversion\"]\n    end\n    \n    divSelRectype --\u003e selRecordtype\n    selRecordtype --\u003e fillSelectRecordTypes\n    fillSelectRecordTypes --\u003e createRectypeSelect\n    createRectypeSelect --\u003e startAction\n```\n\n**Record Type Change Flow**\n\n- **Scope Selection**: Choose records by current type or selection via `selectRecordScope`\n- **Target Type**: Select destination record type from dropdown populated by `_fillSelectRecordTypes()`\n- **Field Mapping**: Automatic handling of compatible field mappings on server-side\n- **Validation**: Input validation in `_startAction()` before API call\n\nSources: [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:197-203](), [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:248-252](), [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:640-642]()\n\n### File Operations\n\nSpecialized batch operations for file management:\n\n| Operation | Purpose systemMgrRepo\n```\n\n**File Operations Implementation**\n\nThe `_createInputElements()` method generates specific UI for each file operation type, with repository integration handled through `SystemMgr.repositoryAction()` calls.\n\nSources: [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:402-473](), [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:85-128](), [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:594-598]()\n\n### Text Processing Operations\n\nAdvanced text manipulation operations implemented in `_createInputElements()`:\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TD\n files (experimental feature)\n\nSources: [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:477-553](), [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:555-573](), [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:604-606](), [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:636-638]()\n\n## Processing Workflow\n\nThe batch operation workflow follows a consistent pattern across all operation types:\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant resultListMenu as \"resultListMenu.js\"\n    participant recordAction as \"recordAction.js\"\n    participant hapi as \"HAPI4\"\n    participant server as \"Server Controller\"\n    participant db as \"MySQL Database\"\n    \n    resultListMenu-\u003e\u003erecordAction: \"menuActionHandler(action)\u003cbr/\u003edetailBatchEditPopup()\"\n    recordAction-\u003e\u003erecordAction: \"_init()\u003cbr/\u003eSetup dialog UI\"\n    recordAction-\u003e\u003erecordAction: \"_fillSelectRecordScope()\u003cbr/\u003e_fillSelectFieldTypes()\"\n    recordAction-\u003e\u003erecordAction: \"_createInputElements()\u003cbr/\u003eDynamic UI generation\"\n    \n    Note over recordAction: User configures operation\n    \n    recordAction-\u003e\u003erecordAction: \"_startAction()\u003cbr/\u003eValidate inputs\"\n    recordAction-\u003e\u003ehapi: \"API call based on action_type\"\n    hapi-\u003e\u003eserver: \"HTTP request with parameters\"\n    server-\u003e\u003edb: \"Execute batch operations\"\n    db--\u003e\u003eserver: \"Operation results\"\n    server--\u003e\u003ehapi: \"JSON response\"\n    hapi--\u003e\u003erecordAction: \"Process response\"\n    recordAction-\u003e\u003erecordAction: \"Display results in div_result\"\n```\n\n**Batch Operation Processing Flow**\n\nThe workflow includes comprehensive error handling through `getFieldValue()` validation and detailed result reporting via the `div_result` container. Each operation constructs a specific request object for the HAPI4 API.\n\nSources: [hclient/widgets/viewers/resultListMenu.js:580-642](), [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:724-1298](), [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:712-719]()\n\n## Record Scope Selection\n\nThe system provides flexible record selection mechanisms through `_fillSelectRecordScope()`:\n\n| Scope Type | Description | Implementation |\n|------------|-------------|----------------|\n| `All` | All records in current result set | `init_scope_type=='all'` |\n| `Current` | Current search results | `currentRecordset.length()` |\n| `Selected` | User-selected records | `currentRecordsetSelection.length` |\n| `Collected` | Records in collection | `currentRecordsetCollected.length` |\n| `By Type` | Records of specific type | `rtyIDs` from `currentRecordset.getRectypes()` |\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TD\n    subgraph \"Scope Selection Logic\"\n        fillSelectRecordScope[\"_fillSelectRecordScope()\u003cbr/\u003ePopulate scope dropdown\"]\n        initScopeType[\"init_scope_type\u003cbr/\u003eInitial scope parameter\"]\n        selectRecordScope[\"selectRecordScope\u003cbr/\u003eDropdown element\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Scope Types\"\n        allRecords[\"All Records\u003cbr/\u003eOption value: 'All'\"]\n        currentResults[\"Current Results\u003cbr/\u003eOption value: 'Current'\"]\n        selectedResults[\"Selected Results\u003cbr/\u003eOption value: 'Selected'\"]\n        collectedResults[\"Collected Results\u003cbr/\u003eOption value: 'Collected'\"]\n        byRecordType[\"By Record Type\u003cbr/\u003eOption value: rty_ID\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Field Type Filtering\"\n        onRecordScopeChange[\"_onRecordScopeChange()\u003cbr/\u003eUpdate field types\"]\n        fillSelectFieldTypes[\"_fillSelectFieldTypes()\u003cbr/\u003eFilter compatible fields\"]\n    end\n    \n    fillSelectRecordScope --\u003e selectRecordScope\n    selectRecordScope --\u003e allRecords\n    selectRecordScope --\u003e currentResults\n    selectRecordScope --\u003e selectedResults\n    selectRecordScope --\u003e collectedResults\n    selectRecordScope --\u003e byRecordType\n    selectRecordScope --\u003e onRecordScopeChange\n    onRecordScopeChange --\u003e fillSelectFieldTypes\n```\n\n**Record Scope Selection Flow**\n\nThe scope selection drives field type availability through `_onRecordScopeChange()`, ensuring only compatible fields are shown for the selected record types. The `allSelectedRectypes` variable tracks record types for Selected/Collected scopes.\n\nSources: [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:133-203](), [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:208-243](), [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:256-319]()\n\n## Implementation Details\n\n### Dynamic UI Generation\n\nThe system dynamically creates form elements based on the selected operation and field types:\n\n- **Field Type Filtering**: Only compatible field types are shown based on operation requirements\n- **Input Widget Creation**: Uses `editing_input.js` widgets for consistent field editing\n- **Validation Logic**: Real-time validation prevents invalid operations\n- **Permission Checking**: UI elements are hidden/disabled based on user permissions\n\n### Error Handling and Results\n\nBatch operations provide detailed feedback:\n\n- **Processing Counts**: Number of records processed, skipped, and failed\n- **Error Details**: Specific error messages for failed operations  \n- **Permission Failures**: Records that couldn't be modified due to access restrictions\n- **Progress Tracking**: Real-time updates during long-running operations\n\n### Server-Side Architecture\n\nThe `RecordsBatch` class handles all server-side processing with methods corresponding to each operation type. It manages database transactions, file system operations, and external API integrations while maintaining data consistency.\n\nSources: [hclient/framecontent/recordAction.js:332-714](), import/export systems, file handling, user/group management, and data integrity features. The data management layer handles the ingestion, transformation, storage, and extraction of scholarly data while maintaining referential integrity and supporting various academic data formats.\n\nFor information about the underlying database structure and schema management, see [Database Structure](#2.3). For details about the user interface components used in data management workflows, see [User Interface](#3).\n\n## Import Systems\n\nHeurist provides multiple import pathways to accommodate different data sources and formats, with sophisticated field mapping and validation capabilities.\n\n### CSV/Delimited Data Import\n\nThe CSV import system supports delimited data formats including CSV, TSV, and KML files through a multi-step workflow.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    UploadFile[\"File Upload\u003cbr/\u003euploadFile input\"] --\u003e ParseAnalyze[\"Parse \u0026 Analyze\u003cbr/\u003e_doParse()\"]\n    ParseAnalyze --\u003e FieldMapping[\"Field Mapping\u003cbr/\u003e_initFieldMapppingTable()\"]\n    FieldMapping --\u003e RecordMatching[\"Record Matching\u003cbr/\u003e_doMatchingInit()\"]\n    RecordMatching --\u003e Validation[\"Validation\u003cbr/\u003e_doPrepare()\"]\n    Validation --\u003e Import[\"Import Records\u003cbr/\u003e_doImport()\"]\n    \n    ParseAnalyze --\u003e SessionMgr[\"Import Session\u003cbr/\u003eSysImportFiles entity\"]\n    SessionMgr --\u003e FieldMapping\n    \n    FieldMapping --\u003e RectypeSeq[\"Record Type Sequence\u003cbr/\u003eimp_session.sequence\"]\n    RectypeSeq --\u003e RecordMatching\n```\n\nThe import process begins with the `hImportRecordsCSV` class in [import/delimited/importRecordsCSV.js:26-470](). The system handles file upload through jQuery FileUpload, automatically detecting encoding and field separators [import/delimited/importRecordsCSV.js:113-258]().\n\n**Sources:** import/delimited/importRecordsCSV.js, import/delimited/importRecordsCSV.php\n\n### HML/JSON Data Import\n\nFor structured data interchange, Heurist supports importing HML (Heurist Markup Language) XML and JSON exports from other Heurist databases.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    FileUpload[\"File Upload\u003cbr/\u003euploadFile\"] --\u003e ParseStructure[\"Parse Structure\u003cbr/\u003ehImportRecords._parseStructure()\"]\n    ParseStructure --\u003e DefSync[\"Definition Sync\u003cbr/\u003e_importDefinitions()\"]\n    DefSync --\u003e RecordImport[\"Record Import\u003cbr/\u003e_importRecords()\"]\n    \n    ParseStructure --\u003e SourceDB[\"Source Database\u003cbr/\u003esource_db_url\"]\n    SourceDB --\u003e RegCheck[\"Registration Check\u003cbr/\u003erectypesInSource\"]\n    RegCheck --\u003e DefSync\n    \n    DefSync --\u003e MissingDefs[\"Missing Definitions\u003cbr/\u003etargetMissed\"]\n    MissingDefs --\u003e RecordImport\n```\n\nThe `hImportRecords` constructor [import/utilities/importRecords.js:23-45]() manages the import workflow, with automatic detection of missing record types and field definitions that need synchronization from the source database.\n\n**Sources:** import/utilities/importRecords.js, import/utilities/importRecords.php\n\n## Export Systems\n\nHeurist provides multiple export formats to support data sharing, archiving, and interoperability with external systems.\n\n### HML Export System\n\nThe HML (Heurist Markup Language) export generates comprehensive XML representations of database content with configurable depth and relationship handling.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph LR\n    QueryEngine[\"Record Query\u003cbr/\u003erecordSearch()\"] --\u003e QueryEngine --\u003e FilterParams[\"Filter Parameters\u003cbr/\u003eRECTYPE_FILTERS\"]\n    FilterParams --\u003e DepthControl[\"Depth Control\u003cbr/\u003eMAX_DEPTH\"]\n    DepthControl --\u003e OutputGeneration\n    \n    OutputGeneration --\u003e XMLOutput[\"XML Output\u003cbr/\u003emakeTag()\"]\n    XMLOutput --\u003e FileOutput[\"File Output\u003cbr/\u003eoutput_file_fd\"]\n```\n\nThe core export logic in [export/xml/flathml.php:451-580]() handles relationship traversal using `findPointers()` and `findRelatedRecords()` functions to build a complete graph of related records with configurable depth limits.\n\n**Sources:** export/xml/flathml.php\n\n### KML Geospatial Export\n\nFor geospatial data sharing, the KML export system converts Heurist's geographic data into Google Earth-compatible format.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    GeoQuery[\"Geographic Query\u003cbr/\u003edtl_Geo field\"] --\u003e WKTConversion[\"WKT to KML\u003cbr/\u003egeoPHP::load()\"]\n    WKTConversion --\u003e PlacemarkGen[\"Placemark Generation\u003cbr/\u003e$geom-\u003eout('kml')\"]\n    \n    TimeData[\"Temporal Data\u003cbr/\u003eDT_DATE, DT_START_DATE\"] --\u003e TimeSpan[\"TimeSpan/TimeStamp\u003cbr/\u003eTemporal::toKML()\"]\n    TimeSpan --\u003e PlacemarkGen\n    \n    FileData[\"KML File References\u003cbr/\u003eDT_KML_FILE\"] --\u003e Document\u003cbr/\u003ekml_file_stream\"]\n```\n\nThe KML export in [export/xml/kml.php:90-279]() uses the geoPHP library to convert WKT geometry data and includes temporal information through the `Temporal` class.\n\n**Sources:** export/xml/kml.php\n\n### Database Archive System\n\nThe archive system creates comprehensive backup packages including SQL dumps, file attachments, and documentation.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    ExportDialog[\"Export Dialog\u003cbr/\u003eexportMyDataPopup.php\"] --\u003e ArchiveOptions[\"Archive Options\u003cbr/\u003eincluderesources, and Scope\n\nThe Import System provides comprehensive data ingestion capabilities for Heurist databases, supporting multiple data formats and external service integration. This system handles CSV/TSV files, XML/JSON/HML formats, file uploads, and synchronization with external bibliographic services like Zotero and FieldHelper.\n\nFor information about data export functionality, see [Export System](#4.2). For file storage and metadata management, see [File Management](#4.3).\n\n## System Architecture\n\nThe Import System follows a client-server architecture with specialized JavaScript classes handling user interaction and PHP controllers managing server-side processing.\n\n### Import System Overview\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"Client Interface Layer\"\n PROCESS\n```\n\n### Import Definition States\n\nThe system tracks various states during definition import:\n\n| State | Description | Action Required |\n|-------|-------------|-----------------|\n| `isAllRecognized` | All types found in target | Proceed to import |\n| `targetMissed` | Record types missing | Download definitions |\n| `targetDtMissed` | Field types missing | Download definitions
