[hserv/utilities/USystem.php](hserv/utilities/USystem.php)\n\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n\n\nThe Export System in Heurist provides functionality for exporting data and database structures in various formats, creating comprehensive backups, and archiving databases. It supports both user-initiated exports through the UI and automated exports through command-line tools and cron jobs. For related functionality that validates data before export, see [Data Management](#4).\n\n## Export Formats and Capabilities\n\nHeurist supports multiple export formats, each serving different purposes:\n\n| Format | Description | Primary Use Cases |\n|--------|-------------|-------------------|\n| HML (Heurist Markup Language) | Custom XML format preserving record relationships | Long-term archiving, data interchange |\n| SQL | Database structure and data as SQL statements | Database migration, backup |\n| TSV (Tab-Separated Values) | Tabular data format | Spreadsheet import, data analysis |\n| ZIP/BZip Archive | Combined package of multiple formats and files | Comprehensive backup, database transfer |\n\n### Export System Architecture\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart TD\n    subgraph \"User Interfaces\"\n        UI[\"UI Export Components\"] --\u003e |\"configures\"| ExportOptions[\"Export Options\"]\n        CLI[\"Command Line Interface\"] --\u003e |\"provides parameters\"| ExportOptions\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Export Core\"\n        ExportOptions --\u003e HMLExport[\"HML Export (flathml.php)\"]\n        ExportOptions --\u003e SQLExport[\"SQL Export\"]\n        ExportOptions --\u003e TSVExport[\"TSV Export\"]\n        ExportOptions --\u003e FileExport[\"File Export\"]\n        \n        HMLExport --\u003e ArchiveBuilder[\"Archive Builder\"]\n        SQLExport --\u003e ArchiveBuilder\n        TSVExport --\u003e ArchiveBuilder\n        FileExport --\u003e ArchiveBuilder\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Data Sources\"\n        HMLExport --\u003e |\"reads\"| Records[(Records)]\n        SQLExport --\u003e |\"reads\"| DBStructure[(DB Structure)]\n        TSVExport --\u003e |\"reads\"| Records\n        FileExport --\u003e |\"reads\"| Files[(Uploaded Files)]\n    end\n    \n    ArchiveBuilder --\u003e |\"produces\"| ArchiveFile[\"Archive File (.zip/.tar.bz2)\"]\n    ArchiveFile --\u003e |\"delivered via\"| Download[\"Download Link\"]\n    ArchiveFile --\u003e |\"or uploaded to\"| Repository[\"External Repository\"]\n```\n\nSources: [export/xml/flathml.php](), [export/dbbackup/exportMyDataPopup.php](), [export/dbbackup/buildArchivePackagesCMD.php]()\n\n## HML (Heurist Markup Language) Export\n\nThe HML export is implemented in `flathml.php` and produces a custom XML format that preserves the relationships between records while avoiding redundancy.\n\n### HML Export Process\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant User as \"User/System\"\n    participant Exporter as \"HML Exporter\"\n    participant DB as \"Database\"\n    participant XML as \"XML Output\"\n    \n    User-\u003e\u003eExporter: Request export with parameters\n    Note right of User: Parameters include:\u003cbr/\u003e- depth\u003cbr/\u003e- linkmode\u003cbr/\u003e- reverse pointers\u003cbr/\u003e- stubs only\u003cbr/\u003e- file content\n    \n    Exporter-\u003e\u003eDB: Query initial records\n    DB-\u003e\u003eExporter: Return records\n    \n    loop For each level of relationships\n        Exporter-\u003e\u003eDB: Query related records\n        DB-\u003e\u003eExporter: Return related records\n        Exporter-\u003e\u003eExporter: Build graph structure\n    end\n    \n    Exporter-\u003e\u003eXML: Generate XML header\n    \n    loop For each record\n        Exporter-\u003e\u003eXML: Output record (outputRecord)\n        loop For each detail\n            Exporter-\u003e\u003eXML: Output detail (outputDetail)\n        end\n    end\n    \n    Exporter-\u003e\u003eXML: Generate XML footer\n    XML-\u003e\u003eUser: Return XML file\n```\n\nSources: [export/xml/flathml.php:512-584](), [export/xml/flathml.php:596-663](), [export/xml/flathml.php:677-772]()\n\n### Key HML Export Parameters\n\n| Parameter | CLI Flag | Description |\n|-----------|----------|-------------|\n| `REVERSE` | `rev` | Include reverse pointer fields (default: yes) |\n| `EXPAND_REV_PTR` | `revexpand` | Expand reverse pointers (default: yes) |\n| `NO_RELATIONSHIPS` | N/A | Don't output relationships |\n| `WOOT` | `woot` | Don't output text content (default: no) |\n| `USEXINCLUDE` | `hinclude` | Use xinclude format for related records |\n| `OUTPUT_STUBS` | `stub` | Output only record header fields |\n| `INCLUDE_FILE_CONTENT` | `fc` | Expand XML file content |\n| `depth` | `depth` | Control how many levels of related records are included |\n| `linkmode` | `linkmode` | Control how links are handled (none, direct, direct_links, all) |\n\nSources: [export/xml/flathml.php:341-401]()\n\n## Database Backup\n\nThe database backup functionality in Heurist creates comprehensive archives of databases, including data, structure, files, and documentation. It's primarily implemented in `exportMyDataPopup.php`.\n\n### Database Backup Process\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart TD\n    ConfigUI[\"Configuration UI\"] --\u003e |\"user selects options\"| BackupOptions[\"Backup Options\"]\n    \n    BackupOptions --\u003e |\"if selected\"| CopyFiles[\"Copy Uploaded Files\"]\n    BackupOptions --\u003e |\"if selected\"| CopyTileStacks[\"Copy Tilestack Images\"]\n    BackupOptions --\u003e |\"if selected\"| ExportHML[\"Export HML\"]\n    BackupOptions --\u003e |\"if selected\"| ExportTSV[\"Export TSV\"]\n    BackupOptions --\u003e |\"always\"| ExportStructureText[\"Export Structure as Text\"]\n    BackupOptions --\u003e |\"always\"| ExportStructureXML[\"Export Structure as XML\"]\n    BackupOptions --\u003e |\"always\"| ExportSQL[\"Export SQL Dump\"]\n    \n    CopyFiles --\u003e TempDir[\"Temporary Directory\"]\n    CopyTileStacks --\u003e TempDir\n    ExportHML --\u003e TempDir\n    ExportTSV --\u003e TempDir\n    ExportStructureText --\u003e TempDir\n    ExportStructureXML --\u003e TempDir\n    ExportSQL --\u003e TempDir\n    \n    TempDir --\u003e |\"zip/tar.bz2\"| Archive[\"Archive File\"]\n    \n    Archive --\u003e |\"download\"| UserDownload[\"User Download\"]\n    Archive --\u003e |\"if repository selected\"| RepositoryUpload[\"Repository Upload\"]\n```\n\nSources: [export/dbbackup/exportMyDataPopup.php:310-314](), [export/dbbackup/exportMyDataPopup.php:443-478](), [export/dbbackup/exportMyDataPopup.php:493-645]()\n\n### Backup Options\n\nThe backup process supports the following options:\n\n1. **Include attached files** - Copies uploaded files into the archive\n2. **Include tiled map images** - Copies tilestack images (typically very large)\n3. **Include HML** - Adds a fully self-documenting HML file\n4. **Include TSV** - Adds a folder containing the database dump in TSV format\n5. **Include documentation** - Adds system documentation for long-term archiving\n6. **Archive format** - ZIP (default) or BZip\n7. **Repository upload** - Option to upload the archive to an external repository (like Nakala)\n\nSources: [export/dbbackup/exportMyDataPopup.php:329-401]()\n\n## Command-line Export Tools\n\nHeurist provides command-line tools for creating exports and backups, primarily for automation and batch processing.\n\n### Build Archive Packages\n\nThe `buildArchivePackagesCMD.php` tool creates archive packages for one or more databases:\n\n```\nsudo php -f buildArchivePackagesCMD.php -- -db=database_1,database_2\nsudo php -f buildArchivePackagesCMD.php -- -db=all -nofiles -nodocs\n```\n\nCommand-line options:\n- `-db=\u003cdatabase_list\u003e` - Specify which database(s) to archive (comma-separated, or \"all\")\n- `-nofiles` - Skip including uploaded files\n- `-nodocs` - Skip including documentation\n- `-nosql` - Skip including SQL export\n- `-nohml` - Skip including HML export\n- `-notsv` - Skip including TSV export\n\nSources: [export/dbbackup/buildArchivePackagesCMD.php:38-90](), [export/dbbackup/buildArchivePackagesCMD.php:160-251]()\n\n### Purge Inactive Databases\n\nThe `purgeInactiveDBs.php` tool archives and optionally deletes inactive databases based on specific criteria:\n\n```\nsudo php -f purgeInactiveDBs.php -- -purge\n```\n\nCriteria for archiving:\n- 3 months inactive with 10 records or less\n- 6 months inactive with 50 records or less\n- 1 year inactive with 200 records or less\n\nSources: [admin/utilities/purgeInactiveDBs.php:79-103](), [admin/utilities/purgeInactiveDBs.php:301-324]()\n\n## Structure Export\n\nHeurist provides functionality to export the database structure in both XML and SQL formats.\n\n### XML Structure Export\n\nThe XML structure export (`getDBStructureAsXML.php`) generates an XML representation of the database structure, including record types, detail types, terms, and other schema elements.\n\nKey features:\n- XML representation of the database structure\n- Option to include user and group information\n- Option to export a subset of the structure by record type, detail type, or term ID\n\nSources: [hserv/structure/export/getDBStructureAsXML.php:80-146](), [hserv/structure/export/getDBStructureAsXML.php:202-224]()\n\n### SQL Structure Export\n\nThe SQL structure export (`getDBStructureAsSQL.php`) generates SQL INSERT statements for recreating the database structure.\n\nKey features:\n- SQL INSERT statements for each definition table\n- Option to include user and group information\n- Option to filter for approved definitions only\n\nSources: [hserv/structure/export/getDBStructureAsSQL.php:66-106](), [hserv/structure/export/getDBStructureAsSQL.php:170-202]()\n\n## Integration with Database Management\n\nThe Export System integrates with the database management functionality in Heurist. The `databaseController.php` file handles various database operations, including exports and backups.\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n    subgraph \"User Interfaces\"\n        UI[\"Web UI\"]\n        CLI[\"Command Line\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Controllers\"\n        DBController[\"Database Controller\"]\n        ExportController[\"Export Controller\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Export Functions\"\n        HML[\"HML Export\"]\n        SQL[\"SQL Export\"]\n        TSV[\"TSV Export\"]\n        Archive[\"Archive Creation\"]\n    end\n    \n    UI --\u003e DBController\n    CLI --\u003e DBController\n    \n    DBController --\u003e |\"calls\"| ExportController\n    ExportController --\u003e HML\n    ExportController --\u003e SQL\n    ExportController --\u003e TSV\n    ExportController --\u003e Archive\n    \n    HML --\u003e |\"produces\"| HMLFile[\"HML File\"]\n    SQL --\u003e |\"produces\"| SQLFile[\"SQL File\"]\n    TSV --\u003e |\"produces\"| TSVFile[\"TSV Files\"]\n    Archive --\u003e |\"packages\"| ArchiveFile[\"Archive File\"]\n```\n\nSources: [hserv/controller/databaseController.php:240-249](), [export/dbbackup/exportMyDataPopup.php:55-146]()\n\n## Automated Exports and Archiving\n\nHeurist includes functionality for automated exports and archiving through cron jobs and scheduled processes.\n\n### Daily Cron Jobs\n\nThe `dailyCronJobs.php` script runs various daily tasks, which can include URL validation related to exports:\n\n```\nsudo php -f dailyCronJobs.php -- url\n```\n\nSources: [admin/setup/dboperations/dailyCronJobs.php:234-299]()\n\n### Database Archiving Notifications\n\nThe `notifyDatabaseArchive.php` script sends notifications to database owners about creating archives of their databases if they've been modified within the last month:\n\n```\nsudo php -f notifyDatabaseArchive.php -- -db=database_1,database_2\n```\n\nSources: [admin/utilities/notifyDatabaseArchive.php:126-185]()\n\n## Export System Data Flow\n\nThe following diagram illustrates the data flow during a complete database backup process:\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant User\n    participant UI as \"Export UI\"\n    participant Exporter as \"Export System\"\n    participant DB as \"Database\"\n    participant FS as \"File System\"\n    \n    User-\u003e\u003eUI: Configure export options\n    User-\u003e\u003eUI: Start export\n    \n    UI-\u003e\u003eExporter: Create temporary directory\n    \n    alt Include files\n        Exporter-\u003e\u003eFS: Copy uploaded files\n        Exporter-\u003e\u003eFS: Copy tiled map images (if selected)\n    end\n    \n    alt Include HML\n        Exporter-\u003e\u003eDB: Query all records\n        DB-\u003e\u003eExporter: Return records data\n        Exporter-\u003e\u003eFS: Write HML file\n    end\n    \n    alt Include TSV\n        Exporter-\u003e\u003eDB: Query records by type\n        DB-\u003e\u003eExporter: Return records data\n        Exporter-\u003e\u003eFS: Write TSV files\n    end\n    \n    Exporter-\u003e\u003eDB: Query database structure\n    DB-\u003e\u003eExporter: Return structure data\n    Exporter-\u003e\u003eFS: Write structure as text\n    Exporter-\u003e\u003eFS: Write structure as XML\n    \n    Exporter-\u003e\u003eDB: Create SQL dump\n    DB-\u003e\u003eExporter: Return SQL data\n    Exporter-\u003e\u003eFS: Write SQL file\n    \n    Exporter-\u003e\u003eFS: Create archive (ZIP/BZip)\n    \n    alt Normal download\n        Exporter-\u003e\u003eUI: Return download link\n        UI-\u003e\u003eUser: Provide download link\n    else Repository upload\n        Exporter-\u003e\u003eUI: Upload to repository\n        UI-\u003e\u003eUser: Show upload result\n    end\n```\n\nSources: [export/dbbackup/exportMyDataPopup.php:461-645]()\n\n## Folder Structure and File Organization\n\nThe Export System functionality is distributed across several directories:\n\n1. **export/xml/** - Contains HML export functionality\n   - `flathml.php` - Core HML export implementation\n\n2. **export/dbbackup/** - Contains database backup functionality\n   - `exportMyDataPopup.php` - UI for database backups\n   - `buildArchivePackagesCMD.php` - Command-line tool for building archive packages\n\n3. **hserv/structure/export/** - Contains structure export functionality\n   - `getDBStructureAsXML.php` - XML structure export\n   - `getDBStructureAsSQL.php` - SQL structure export\n\n4. **admin/utilities/** - Contains utility scripts\n   - `purgeInactiveDBs.php` - Purges and archives inactive databases\n   - `cleanupFoldersDBs.php` - Cleans up temporary files and logs\n   - `notifyDatabaseArchive.php` - Sends database archive notifications\n\n5. **admin/verification/** - Contains verification tools\n   - `checkRecURL.php` - Checks record URLs for validity\n\nSources: File paths from the provided code files\n\n## Conclusion\n\nThe Heurist Export System provides comprehensive functionality for exporting data and database structures in various formats, creating backups, and archiving databases. This system handles both local file storage on the Heurist server and references to external files via URLs, including specialized support for tiled image stacks and integration with external repositories.\n\nThis document covers file upload workflows, storage mechanisms, search and management interfaces, and integration with external repositories. For information about data import/export operations, see [Import System](#4.1) and [Export System](#4.2). For media display and viewing capabilities, see [Record Viewing](#3.3).\n\n## System Architecture\n\nThe File Management system is built around the `manageRecUploadedFiles` widget, which provides a comprehensive interface for file operations, and the `searchRecUploadedFiles` widget for filtering and finding files.\n\n### Core File Management Architecture\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph Edit\u003cbr/\u003eMetadata Management\"]\n        FD[\"File Delete\u003cbr/\u003eWith Reference Check\"]\n        BO[\"Batch Operations\u003cbr/\u003eSelect, Delete, Export\"]\n    end\n    \n Stacks\u003cbr/\u003eZoomify, IIIF\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Database Layer\"\n        RUF[(\"recUploadedFiles\u003cbr/\u003eFile Records\")]\n        FXM[(\"defFileExtToMimetype\u003cbr/\u003eMIME Type Mapping\")]\n    end\n    \n    MRF --\u003e FU\n    MRF --\u003e FE  \n    MRF --\u003e FD\n    MRF --\u003e BO\n    SRF --\u003e MRF\n    \n    FU symbology, and configuration. The `hMapDocument` class manages these complex data structures:\n\nThe system supports multiple coordinate reference systems (CRS) through the `defineCRS()` function, with special handling for `L.CRS.Simple` for image-based mapping and standard geographic projections like `EPSG3857` and `EPSG4326`.\n\nLayer visibility and symbology are controlled through the map manager, which provides an accordion-style interface for base maps, map documents, temporary layers, and search results. The `HMapManager` class coordinates these interactions.\n\n**Sources:** [viewers/map/mapping.js:634-682](), [viewers/map/mapManager.js:126-276](), [viewers/map/mapDocument.js:37-67]()\n\n## CMS Heurist Mapping System provides comprehensive geospatial visualization capabilities, including interactive maps with timeline integration, layer management, drawing tools, and story mapping functionality. This system enables users to visualize geographic data from Heurist records, create map documents with multiple layers, and build interactive story maps that combine spatial and temporal data.\n\nFor information about the broader User Interface framework that contains mapping widgets, see [User Interface](#3). For details about the CMS website generation system that can embed maps, see [CMS Website Generator](#5.2).\n\n## Architecture Overview\n\nThe mapping system follows a layered architecture with the main `mapping` widget at its core, supported by specialized managers for layers, documents, and UI controls.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"Client Applications\"\n [viewers/map/mapManager.js:1-200](), [hclient/widgets/viewers/app_timemap.js:1-100](), [viewers/map/map.php:1-50]()\n\n## Core Mapping Widget\n\nThe `mapping` jQuery widget serves as the central component that orchestrates all mapping functionality. It manages the Leaflet map instance, coordinates with other components, and handles user interactions.\n\n### Key Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Function | Key Methods |\n|-----------|----------|-------------|\n| **Map Instance Management** | Creates and manages Leaflet map | `_init()`, `invalidateSize()` |\n| **Layer Coordination** | Adds/removes layers, manages visibility | `addSearchResult()`, `addRecordSet()`, `addTileLayer()` |\n| **Drawing Tools** | Handles digitizing and geometry editing | `drawLoadGeometry()`, `drawGetWkt()`, `drawClearAll()` |\n| **Base Map Support** | Manages tile providers and base layers | `loadBaseMap()`, `getBaseMapProviders()` |\n| **CRS Management** | Coordinate reference system handling | `defineCRS()`, `getCurrentCRS()` |\n\n### Core Widget Structure\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    WIDGET_CREATE[\"_create()\u003cbr/\u003eWidget Constructor\"]\n    WIDGET_INIT[\"_init()\u003cbr/\u003eLayout \u0026 Map System\n\nThe `hMapDocument` class manages collections of map layers organized into documents. Map documents can contain multiple layers with different data sources, symbology, and visibility settings.\n\n### Document and Layer Hierarchy\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"Record Types\"\n [hclient/widgets/cms/websiteScriptAndStyles.php](hclient/widgets/cms/websiteScriptAndStyles.php)\n\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n\n\nThe CMS Website Generator is a comprehensive content management system that creates dynamic websites from structured data records in Heurist. It provides a visual editor for creating and managing website content, supports templating and custom layouts, and integrates with Heurist's widget system to display database content dynamically.\n\nThis document covers the technical architecture, components, and workflows of the CMS system. For information about the mapping system used within CMS pages, see [Mapping System](#5.1). For details about data visualization widgets that can be embedded in CMS pages, see [Data Analysis and Visualization](#5.3).\n\n## System Overview\n\nThe CMS Website Generator operates through a record-based architecture where websites are defined by specific record types in the Heurist database. The system generates static-like websites with dynamic content capabilities through JavaScript widgets and database queries.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"CMS_Records\"[\"CMS Database Records\"]\n        RT_CMS_HOME[\"RT_CMS_HOME\u003cbr/\u003eWebsite Home Page\"]\n        RT_CMS_MENU[\"RT_CMS_MENU\u003cbr/\u003eMenu/Page Items\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Template_System\"[\"Template Processing\"]\n        websiteRecord[\"websiteRecord.php\u003cbr/\u003eTemplate Controller\"]\n        cmsTemplate[\"cmsTemplate.php\u003cbr/\u003eDefault Template\"]\n        customTemplate[\"Custom Templates\u003cbr/\u003eUser-defined\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Editor_Interface\"[\"CMS Editor\"]\n        editCMS2[\"editCMS2.js\u003cbr/\u003eMain Editor\"]\n        editCMS_ElementCfg[\"editCMS_ElementCfg.js\u003cbr/\u003eElement Configuration\"]\n        editCMS_SiteMenu[\"editCMS_SiteMenu.js\u003cbr/\u003eSite Structure\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph layoutMgr[\"LayoutMgr\u003cbr/\u003eWidget Management\"]\n        widgets[\"Heurist Widgets\u003cbr/\u003eDynamic Content\"]\n    end\n    \n    RT_CMS_HOME --\u003e websiteRecord\n    RT_CMS_MENU Architecture\n\n### Database Structure\n\nThe CMS system is built around three primary record types that define the website structure:\n\n| Record Type | Purpose | Key Fields |\n|-------------|---------|------------|\n| `RT_CMS_HOME` | Website home page and global settings | `DT_NAME`, `DT_EXTENDED_DESCRIPTION`, `DT_CMS_CSS`, `DT_CMS_SCRIPT` |\n| `RT_CMS_MENU` | Menu items and individual pages | `DT_NAME`, `DT_EXTENDED_DESCRIPTION`, `DT_CMS_TOP_MENU`, `DT_CMS_MENU` |\n| `RT_CMS_HEADER` | Custom header content | `DT_EXTENDED_DESCRIPTION` |\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"Database_Schema\"[\"CMS Database Schema\"]\n        CMS_HOME[\"`RT_CMS_HOME\n        Website Configuration\n        - DT_NAME: Site Title\n        - DT_EXTENDED_DESCRIPTION: Home Content\n        - DT_NAME: Page Title\n        - DT_EXTENDED_DESCRIPTION: Page Content\n        - DT_CMS_MENU: Child Menu Links\n        - DT_CMS_PAGETITLE: Show Page Title\n        - DT_CMS_PAGETYPE: Page Type`\"]\n        \n        CMS_HEADER[\"`RT_CMS_HEADER\n        Custom Header\n        - DT_EXTENDED_DESCRIPTION: Header HTML`\"]\n    end\n    \n    subgraph \"Relationships\"[\"Record Relationships\"]\n        HOME_TO_MENU[\"DT_CMS_TOP_MENU\u003cbr/\u003eLinks to root menu items\"]\n        MENU_TO_MENU[\"DT_CMS_MENU\u003cbr/\u003eLinks to child menu items\"]\n        HOME_TO_HEADER[\"DT_CMS_HEADER\u003cbr/\u003eLinks to custom header\"]\n    end\n    \n    CMS_HOME --\u003e HOME_TO_MENU\n    HOME_TO_MENU --\u003e CMS_MENU\n    CMS_MENU --\u003e MENU_TO_MENU\n    MENU_TO_MENU --\u003e CMS_MENU\n    CMS_HOME --\u003e HOME_TO_HEADER\n    HOME_TO_HEADER --\u003e CMS_HEADER\n```\n\nSources: [hclient/widgets/cms/websiteRecord.php:114-157](), [hclient/widgets/cms/editCMS_SiteMenu.js:42-48]()\n\n### Website Generation Workflow\n\nThe website generation process follows a structured workflow from database records to rendered web [hclient/widgets/cms/websiteScriptAndStyles.php:27-42](), [hclient/widgets/cms/websiteRecord.php:106-112]()\n\n## Template System\n\n### Default Template Structure\n\nThe default CMS template (`cmsTemplate.php`) provides a standardized layout structure with predefined containers for content:\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"Template_Layout\"[\"cmsTemplate.php Layout\"]\n specialized data analysis and visualization capabilities within Heurist, including crosstab analysis tools and network diagram visualization. These features enable users to perform statistical analysis and visual exploration of relationships within their data.\n\nFor mapping and geospatial visualization, see [Mapping System](#5.1). For basic record viewing and reports, see [Record Viewing](#3.3).\n\n## Overview\n\nThe data analysis and visualization system provides two main analytical capabilities:\n\n1. **Crosstab Analysis** - Statistical cross-tabulation with configurable intervals and aggregation modes\n2. **Network Visualization** - Graph-based visualization of record relationships and connections\n\nBoth systems integrate with the core search and record management infrastructure to provide real-time analysis of result sets.\n\n## Crosstab Analysis System\n\n### Architecture\n\nThe crosstab analysis system consists of client-side JavaScript for UI management and server-side PHP for data processing and SQL generation.\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    UI[\"CrosstabsAnalysis Class\u003cbr/\u003ecrosstabs.js\"] Analysis\"]\n        Controller --\u003e Pointers[\"recordSearchDistinctPointers()\u003cbr/\u003eReference Resolution\"]\n        Controller --\u003e CrossTab[\"getCrossTab()\u003cbr/\u003eAggregation [admin/setup/dboperations/dailyCronJobs.php:1-400]()\n\n## Database Operations\n\n### Database Statistics and Management\n\nThe `dbStatistics.php` script provides comprehensive database information and management capabilities:\n\n```mermaid\ngraph LR\n    DB_LIST[\"Database List\u003cbr/\u003emysql__getdatabases4()\"] --\u003e STATS_CALC[\"Statistics Calculation\u003cbr/\u003emysql__select_val()\"]\n    STATS_CALC --\u003e DATA_TABLE[\"DataTable Display\u003cbr/\u003e_dataTableParams\"]\n    DATA_TABLE --\u003e DELETE_FUNC[\"Database Deletion\u003cbr/\u003edeleteDatabases()\"]\n    DELETE_FUNC --\u003e PWD_CHECK[\"Password Verification\u003cbr/\u003echeckPassword()\"]\n    PWD_CHECK --\u003e DELETE_REQ[\"Delete Request\u003cbr/\u003epostDeleteRequest()\"]\n```\n\nThe system tracks various database metrics including record counts, file sizes, modification dates, and ownership information. Key functions include:\n\n- `mysql__select_val()` - Executes queries to gather database statistics\n- `validateDatabases()` - Checks database integrity by verifying required tables\n- `deleteDatabases()` - Initiates database deletion with password verification\n- `getSelectedDatabases()` - Retrieves checked databases for bulk operations\n\nSources: [admin/describe/dbStatistics.php:49-56](), [admin/describe/dbStatistics.php:412-432](), [admin/describe/dbStatistics.php:430-464]()\n\n### Database Deletion Process\n\nThe database deletion workflow involves multiple verification steps and cleanup operations:\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant UI as \"Admin Interface\"\n    participant DELETE as \"deleteDB.php\"\n    participant UTILS as \"DbUtils\"\n    participant EMAIL as \"Email System\"\n    \n    UI-\u003e\u003eDELETE: Delete Request + Password\n    DELETE-\u003e\u003eDELETE: databaseValidateName()\n    DELETE-\u003e\u003eDELETE: verifyActionPassword()\n    DELETE-\u003e\u003eUTILS: databaseDrop()\n    UTILS-\u003e\u003eUTILS: Create Archive\n    UTILS-\u003e\u003eUTILS: Drop Database\n    UTILS-\u003e\u003eUTILS: Remove Filestore\n    DELETE-\u003e\u003eEMAIL: sendEmailDatabaseDelete()\n    EMAIL-\u003e\u003eUI: Deletion Complete\n```\n\nThe `DbUtils::databaseDrop()` function handles the complete removal process including archival options and filestore cleanup.\n\nSources: [admin/setup/dboperations/deleteDB.php:34-150](), [admin/setup/dboperations/deleteDB.php:114-120]()\n\n### Duplicate Record Management\n\nThe `combineDuplicateRecords.php` script provides functionality to merge duplicate records:\n\n| Function | Purpose | Parameters |\n|----------|---------|------------|\n| `keep_bib()` | Selects master record | Record ID |\n| `delete_bib()` | Marks record for deletion | Record ID |\n| `do_fix_dupe()` | Executes merge operation | Master + duplicate IDs |\n| `detail_get_html_input_str()` | Generates merge UI | Detail array, repeat count |\n\nThe merge process transfers bookmarks, tags, relationships, and references from duplicate records to the master record before deletion.\n\nSources: [admin/verification/combineDuplicateRecords.php:115-155](), [admin/verification/combineDuplicateRecords.php:681-750]()\n\n## Bulk Email System\n\n### System Architecture\n\nThe bulk email functionality is implemented through three main components:\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TB\n    subgraph \"User Interface Layer\"\n |\n|--------|---------|----------------|\n| `processFormData()` | Validates input | Database validation, user validation |\n| `createUserList()` | Builds recipient list | Database queries, email validation |\n| `createRecordsList()` | Gathers database metrics | Record counts, modification dates |\n| `constructEmails()` | Sends emails | Template processing, delivery |\n\nThe system supports Scope\n\nThe Database Administration system provides comprehensive tools for maintaining data integrity, verifying database consistency, and performing bulk maintenance operations in Heurist databases. This system encompasses verification utilities, rebuild operations, and long-running task management to ensure database health and performance.\n\nFor user management and permissions, see [User Management](#6.2). For general system configuration, see [Configuration System](#2.2).\n\n## Database Administration Architecture\n\nThe database administration system provides comprehensive tools for maintaining system handles time-intensive operations through a specialized wrapper that provides progress tracking and user feedback:\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart TD\n    USER_REQUEST[User Initiates Operation] --\u003e LONG_INIT[longOperationInit.php\u003cbr/\u003eOperation Router]\n    \n    LONG_INIT --\u003e OPERATION_TYPE{Operation Type}\n    \n    OPERATION_TYPE --\u003e|titles| REBUILD_TITLES[rebuildRecordTitles.php]\n    OPERATION_TYPE --\u003e|calcfields| REBUILD_CALC[rebuildCalculatedFields.php]\n    OPERATION_TYPE --\u003e|files| FILE_ERRORS[listUploadedFilesErrors.php]\n    OPERATION_TYPE --\u003e|urls| CHECK_URLS[checkRecURL.php]\n    OPERATION_TYPE --\u003e|entrymask| REBUILD_MASKS[rebuildEntryMasks.php]\n    \n BATCH_PROCESS --\u003e TERM_CHECK\n    TERM_CHECK --\u003e SESSION_CLEANUP\n```\n\n### Progress Session Management\n\nLong-running operations use session-based progress tracking implemented in the `progressSession()` function:\n\n- **Initialization**: Creates progress session with total record count\n- **Updates**: Reports progress every 25 processed records\n- **Termination**: Checks for user cancellation requests\n- **Cleanup**: Removes session files upon completion\n\nSources: [admin/verification/rebuildRecordTitles.php:328-348](), [admin/verification/longOperationInit.php:32-61]()\n\n## Rebuild Operations\n\n### Record Title Reconstruction\n\nThe `rebuildRecordTitles.php` system rebuilds constructed titles using title masks:\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TD\n    START[Start Title Rebuild] --\u003e GET_RECORDS[Retrieve Records\u003cbr/\u003eRandom Order Processing]\n    GET_RECORDS --\u003e LOAD_MASKS[Load Title Masks\u003cbr/\u003edefRecTypes.rty_TitleMask]\n    \n    LOAD_MASKS --\u003e PROCESS_LOOP[Process Each Record]\n    PROCESS_LOOP --\u003e EXECUTE_MASK[TitleMask::execute\u003cbr/\u003eGenerate New Title]\n    \n    EXECUTE_MASK --\u003e COMPARE[Compare with Existing\u003cbr/\u003erec_Title]\n    COMPARE --\u003e|Different| UPDATE_TITLE[Update Record Title\u003cbr/\u003eMySQL Update]\n    COMPARE --\u003e|Blank| CHECK_RELATIONSHIP[Check for Relationship Record\u003cbr/\u003eupdateRaltionhipForBlankTitle]\n    COMPARE --\u003e|Same| SKIP[Skip Update\u003cbr/\u003eIncrement unchanged_count]\n    \n    UPDATE_TITLE --\u003e TRACK_PROGRESS[Update Progress Session]\n    CHECK_RELATIONSHIP --\u003e TRACK_PROGRESS\n    SKIP --\u003e TRACK_PROGRESS\n    \n    TRACK_PROGRESS --\u003e CHECK_TERMINATION{User Terminated?}\n    CHECK_TERMINATION --\u003e|No| PROCESS_LOOP\n    CHECK_TERMINATION --\u003e|Yes| CLEANUP[Cleanup and Report]\n```\n\nThe rebuild process handles three outcomes:\n- **Updated**: Title changed and updated in database\n- **Unchanged**: Title identical to existing value\n- **Blank**: New title would be empty (preserves existing title)\n\nSources: [admin/verification/rebuildRecordTitles.php:234-324](), [admin/verification/rebuildRecordTitles.php:270-295]()\n\n### Calculated Fields Reconstruction\n\nThe `rebuildCalculatedFields.php` system recalculates formula-based fields using the `recordUpdateCalcFields()` function from the record modification system. This operation processes all calculated field types and reports changes, clearances, and errors.\n\nSources: [admin/verification/rebuildCalculatedFields.php:43-75]()\n\n## File System The Export System in Heurist provides functionality for exporting data and database structures in various formats, creating comprehensive backups, and archiving databases. It supports both user-initiated exports through the UI and automated exports through command-line tools and cron jobs. For related functionality that validates data before export, see Data Management.
Export Formats and Capabilities
Heurist supports multiple export formats, each serving different purposes:






























FormatDescriptionPrimary Use CasesHML (Heurist Markup Language)Custom XML format preserving record relationshipsLong-term archiving, data interchangeSQLDatabase structure and data as SQL statementsDatabase migration, backupTSV (Tab-Separated Values)Tabular data formatSpreadsheet import, data analysisZIP/BZip ArchiveCombined package of multiple formats and filesComprehensive backup, database transfer
Export System Architecture

HML (Heurist Markup Language) Export
The HML export is implemented in flathml.php and produces a custom XML format that preserves the relationships between records while avoiding redundancy.
HML Export Process

Database Backup
The database backup functionality in Heurist creates comprehensive archives of databases, including data, structure, files, and documentation. It's primarily implemented in exportMyDataPopup.php.
Database Backup Process

Backup Options
The backup process supports the following options:

Include attached files - Copies uploaded files into the archive
Include tiled map images - Copies tilestack images (typically very large)
Include HML - Adds a fully self-documenting HML file
Include TSV - Adds a folder containing the database dump in TSV format
Include documentation - Adds system documentation for long-term archiving
Archive format - ZIP (default) or BZip
Repository upload - Option to upload the archive to an external repository (like Nakala)

Sources: export/dbbackup/exportMyDataPopup.php329-401
Command-line Export Tools
Heurist provides command-line tools for creating exports and backups, primarily for automation and batch processing.
Build Archive Packages
The buildArchivePackagesCMD.php tool creates archive packages for one or more databases:
sudo php -f buildArchivePackagesCMD.php -- -db=database_1,database_2
sudo php -f buildArchivePackagesCMD.php -- -db=all -nofiles -nodocs

Command-line options:

-db=<database_list> - Specify which database(s) to archive (comma-separated, or "all")
-nofiles - Skip including uploaded files
-nodocs - Skip including documentation
-nosql - Skip including SQL export
-nohml - Skip including HML export
-notsv - Skip including TSV export

Sources: export/dbbackup/buildArchivePackagesCMD.php38-90 export/dbbackup/buildArchivePackagesCMD.php160-251
Purge Inactive Databases
The purgeInactiveDBs.php tool archives and optionally deletes inactive databases based on specific criteria:
sudo php -f purgeInactiveDBs.php -- -purge

Criteria for archiving:

3 months inactive with 10 records or less
6 months inactive with 50 records or less
1 year inactive with 200 records or less

Sources: admin/utilities/purgeInactiveDBs.php79-103 admin/utilities/purgeInactiveDBs.php301-324
Structure Export
Heurist provides functionality to export the database structure in both XML and SQL formats.
XML Structure Export
The XML structure export (getDBStructureAsXML.php) generates an XML representation of the database structure, including record types, detail types, terms, and other schema elements.
Key features:

XML representation of the database structure
Option to include user and group information
Option to export a subset of the structure by record type, detail type, or term ID

Sources: hserv/structure/export/getDBStructureAsXML.php80-146 hserv/structure/export/getDBStructureAsXML.php202-224
SQL Structure Export
The SQL structure export (getDBStructureAsSQL.php) generates SQL INSERT statements for recreating the database structure.
Key features:

SQL INSERT statements for each definition table
Option to include user and group information
Option to filter for approved definitions only

Sources: hserv/structure/export/getDBStructureAsSQL.php66-106 hserv/structure/export/getDBStructureAsSQL.php170-202
Integration with Database Management
The Export System integrates with the database management functionality in Heurist. The databaseController.php file handles various database operations, including exports and backups.

Automated Exports and Archiving
Heurist includes functionality for automated exports and archiving through cron jobs and scheduled processes.
Daily Cron Jobs
The dailyCronJobs.php script runs various daily tasks, which can include URL validation related to exports:
sudo php -f dailyCronJobs.php -- url

Sources: admin/setup/dboperations/dailyCronJobs.php234-299
Database Archiving Notifications
The notifyDatabaseArchive.php script sends notifications to database owners about creating archives of their databases if they've been modified within the last month:
sudo php -f notifyDatabaseArchive.php -- -db=database_1,database_2

Sources: admin/utilities/notifyDatabaseArchive.php126-185

Folder Structure and File Organization
The Export System functionality is distributed across several directories:


export/xml/ - Contains HML export functionality

flathml.php - Core HML export implementation



export/dbbackup/ - Contains database backup functionality

exportMyDataPopup.php - UI for database backups
buildArchivePackagesCMD.php - Command-line tool for building archive packages



hserv/structure/export/ - Contains structure export functionality

getDBStructureAsXML.php - XML structure export
getDBStructureAsSQL.php - SQL structure export



admin/utilities/ - Contains utility scripts

purgeInactiveDBs.php - Purges and archives inactive databases
cleanupFoldersDBs.php - Cleans up temporary files and logs
notifyDatabaseArchive.php - Sends database archive notifications



admin/verification/ - Contains verification tools

checkRecURL.php - Checks record URLs for validity



Sources: File paths from the provided code files
Conclusion
The Heurist Export System provides comprehensive functionality for exporting data and database structures in various formats, creating backups, and archiving databases. It supports both user-initiated exports through the UI and automated exports through command-line tools and cron jobs. The system is designed to be flexible and customizable, with various options for controlling what data is exported and how it's structured.DismissRefresh this wikiEnter email to refreshOn this pageExport SystemExport Formats and CapabilitiesExport System ArchitectureHML (Heurist Markup Language) ExportHML Export ProcessKey HML Export ParametersDatabase BackupDatabase Backup ProcessBackup OptionsCommand-line Export ToolsBuild Archive PackagesPurge Inactive DatabasesStructure ExportXML Structure ExportSQL Structure ExportIntegration with Database ManagementAutomated Exports and ArchivingDaily Cron JobsDatabase Archiving NotificationsExport System Data FlowFolder Structure and File OrganizationConclusionAsk Devin about HeuristNetwork/heuristDeep Research
